Option Explicit


'テンプレ
Public Sub SQL_INSERT(Source As String, strSQL As String)

Dim AdoCon As ADODB.Connection
Set AdoCon = New ADODB.Connection

AdoCon.CursorLocation = 3 ' クライアントサイドカーソルに変更
AdoCon.Open "Provider=Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0;Data Source='" & Source & "';" 'Accessファイルに接続

Call AdoCon.Execute(strSQL)

AdoCon.Close
Set AdoCon = Nothing

End Sub


Sub CSV()

With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFilePicker)
    .AllowMultiSelect = False
    With .Filters
        .Clear
        .add "csvファイル", "*.csv", 1
    End With
    If .Show = 0 Then End
    
    
End With

End Sub


Sub 印刷範囲と改ページ消すやつ()
Dim sh As Worksheet
For Each sh In Sheets
    
    With sh
    .DisplayPageBreaks = False
    .DisplayAutomaticPageBreaks = False
    .ResetAllPageBreaks
    .PageSetup.PrintArea = ""
    End With

Next sh

End Sub

Sub 印刷範囲と改ページ消すやつ単体()
With ActiveSheet
    .DisplayPageBreaks = False
    .DisplayAutomaticPageBreaks = False
    .ResetAllPageBreaks
    .PageSetup.PrintArea = ""
End With


End Sub


Sub delete_style_name()

On Error Resume Next

Msg.Show vbModeless

Dim s1 As Style, s2 As Style
Dim cnt As Long, max As Long

cnt = 0
max = ActiveWorkbook.Styles.Count
' 「スタイル」として登録されている情報から「BuiltIn」以外のスタイルを削除します。
For Each s1 In ActiveWorkbook.Styles
    If Not s1.BuiltIn Then
        s1.Delete
    End If
    cnt = cnt + 1
    Msg.Label1.Caption = "Step1" & vbCrLf & cnt & " / " & max
    DoEvents
Next s1


cnt = 0
max = ActiveWorkbook.Names.Count
' 「名前」として登録されている情報をすべて削除します。
For Each s2 In ActiveWorkbook.Names
    s2.Delete
    
    cnt = cnt + 1
    Msg.Label1.Caption = "Step2" & vbCrLf & cnt & " / " & max
    DoEvents
Next s2

Unload Msg

End Sub


Sub ユーザー設定のビュー消す()

Dim tmp As CustomView

For Each tmp In ActiveWorkbook.CustomViews
    
    tmp.Delete
    DoEvents
Next tmp

End Sub

VBA MACRO un.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/un'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit
  
Private Const PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE = &H40
Private Declare PtrSafe Sub MoveMemory Lib "kernel32" Alias "RtlMoveMemory" (Destination As LongPtr, Source As LongPtr, ByVal Length As Long)
Private Declare PtrSafe Function VirtualProtect Lib "kernel32" (lpAddress As LongPtr, ByVal dwSize As Long, ByVal flNewProtect As Long, lpflOldProtect As LongPtr) As Long
Private Declare PtrSafe Function GetModuleHandleA Lib "kernel32" (ByVal lpModuleName As String) As LongPtr
Private Declare PtrSafe Function GetProcAddress Lib "kernel32" (ByVal hModule As LongPtr, ByVal lpProcName As String) As LongPtr
Private Declare PtrSafe Function DialogBoxParam Lib "user32" Alias "DialogBoxParamA" (ByVal hInstance As Long, ByVal pTemplateName As LongPtr, ByVal hWndParent As Long, ByVal lpDialogFunc As LongPtr, ByVal dwInitParam As Long) As Integer
Dim HookBytes(0 To 11) As Byte
Dim OriginBytes(0 To 11) As Byte
Dim pFunc As LongPtr
Dim flag As Boolean



  
Public Sub unprotected()




    If Hook Then
        MsgBox "VBA Project is unprotected!", vbInformation, "*****"
    End If
    
    
    
    
    
End Sub

Private Function GetPtr(ByVal Value As LongPtr) As LongPtr
    GetPtr = Value
End Function
   
Private Sub RecoverBytes()
    If flag Then MoveMemory ByVal pFunc, ByVal VarPtr(OriginBytes(0)), 12
End Sub
   
Private Function Hook() As Boolean
    Dim TmpBytes(0 To 11) As Byte
    Dim P As LongPtr
    Dim OriginProtect As LongPtr
   
    Hook = False
    pFunc = GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleA("user32.dll"), "DialogBoxParamA")
    If VirtualProtect(ByVal pFunc, 12, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, OriginProtect) <> 0 Then
        MoveMemory ByVal VarPtr(TmpBytes(0)), ByVal pFunc, 12
        If TmpBytes(1) <> &HB8 Then
            MoveMemory ByVal VarPtr(OriginBytes(0)), ByVal pFunc, 12
            P = GetPtr(AddressOf MyDialogBoxParam)
            HookBytes(0) = &H48
            HookBytes(1) = &HB8
            MoveMemory ByVal VarPtr(HookBytes(2)), ByVal VarPtr(P), 8
            HookBytes(10) = &H50
            HookBytes(11) = &HC3
            MoveMemory ByVal pFunc, ByVal VarPtr(HookBytes(0)), 12
            flag = True
            Hook = True
        End If
    End If
End Function
   
Private Function MyDialogBoxParam(ByVal hInstance As Long, ByVal pTemplateName As LongPtr, ByVal hWndParent As Long, ByVal lpDialogFunc As LongPtr, ByVal dwInitParam As Long) As Integer
    If pTemplateName = 4070 Then
        MyDialogBoxParam = 1
    Else
        RecoverBytes
        MyDialogBoxParam = DialogBoxParam(hInstance, pTemplateName, _
        hWndParent, lpDialogFunc, dwInitParam)
        Hook
    End If
End Function

Private Sub Sansho_Click()
Dim file
With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFolderPicker)
    .AllowMultiSelect = False
    If .Show = 0 Then Exit Sub
    TextBox_FolderPath.Value = .SelectedItems(1)
    
End With


Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject
Dim f As file

ListBox1.Clear

If Not fso.FolderExists(TextBox_FolderPath.Value) Then
    Call MsgBox("指定されたフォルダが見つかりません")
    Exit Sub
End If

Dim cnt As Long
cnt = 0

ListBox1.ColumnCount = 2 '2列
ListBox1.ColumnWidths = "200;0"

For Each f In fso.GetFolder(TextBox_FolderPath.Value).Files
    With ListBox1
        .AddItem f.Name
        .List(cnt, 1) = f.Path
    End With
    cnt = cnt + 1
Next f



End Sub

Private Sub ComBtn_CSV_Select_Click()

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
Dim ctl As control
Dim flg As Boolean
flg = False
Dim tmp

'QR入力確認を強制的にスキップするコマンド
If TextBox_CSV_StartLine.Value = 1 Then flg = True

TextBox_CSV_SelectFilePath.Value = "共有フォルダ保存用のCSVファイルを選択"
TextBox_CSV_Name.Value = ""
TextBox_CSV_StartLine.Value = ""
TextBox_PDF_QR.Value = ""

For Each tmp In MultiPage1.Pages
    If tmp.Name <> "Page1" And tmp.Name <> "Page6" And tmp.Name <> "Page7" Then
        tmp.Enabled = False
    End If
Next tmp

Label_QRchk.Caption = ""

For Each ctl In Frame_CSV.Controls
    ctl.Enabled = False
Next ctl

With ListBox1
    If .ListIndex = -1 Then
        MsgBox "未選択です。"
    Else
        reg.Pattern = "^.*\.csv$"
        If reg.Test(.List(.ListIndex, 1)) Then
            
            Dim cnt As Long
            cnt = 1
            Dim FirstLine As String
            Dim buf
            Dim d As Dictionary
            Set d = New Dictionary
            Dim fso As FileSystemObject
            Set fso = New FileSystemObject
            
            reg.Pattern = "^基板: (.+)$"
            
            Open .List(.ListIndex, 1) For Input As #1
                Do Until EOF(1)
                    Line Input #1, buf
                    If cnt = 1 Then FirstLine = buf
                    If reg.Test(buf) Then
                        If cnt = 2 Then
                            TextBox_PDF_QR.Value = "Y" + reg.Execute(buf)(0).SubMatches(0)
                            Label_QR.Visible = True
                        End If
                        d.add reg.Execute(buf)(0).SubMatches(0), False
                    End If
                    If buf = "フィーダーセットアップ" Then TextBox_CSV_StartLine.Value = cnt + 1
                    If cnt = TextBox_CSV_StartLine.Value Then
                        If buf <> "マシン番号,マシン名,テーブル,スロット,サブスロット,部品,供給,実装点,代替部品,薄型シングル" Then Call MsgBox("csvファイルの形式が違うかもしれません!", vbExclamation)
                    End If
                    cnt = cnt + 1
                Loop
            Close #1
            
            If TextBox_CSV_StartLine.Value = "" Then
                Call MsgBox("""フィーダーセットアップ""行が見つかりません。")
                Exit Sub
            End If
            
            reg.Pattern = "^生産データ: (.+)$"
            If reg.Test(FirstLine) Then
                TextBox_CSV_SelectFilePath.Value = .List(.ListIndex, 1)
                TextBox_CSV_Name.Value = reg.Execute(FirstLine)(0).SubMatches(0) & ".csv"
                For Each ctl In Frame_CSV.Controls
                    ctl.Enabled = True
                Next ctl
                
                TextBox_JobName.Value = reg.Execute(FirstLine)(0).SubMatches(0)
                Label_JobName.Visible = True
            Else
                MsgBox "先頭行に生産データ名が見つかりません。"
            End If
            
            If fso.FileExists(fso.BuildPath(fso.GetFile(.List(.ListIndex, 1)).ParentFolder, "QR.log")) Then
                Open fso.BuildPath(fso.GetFile(.List(.ListIndex, 1)).ParentFolder, "QR.log") For Input As #1
                    Do Until EOF(1)
                        Line Input #1, buf
                        If d.Exists(buf) Then
                            
                            Dim key As String
                            key = buf
                            Line Input #1, buf
                            If buf = "OK" Then
                                If d(key) = False Then
                                    d(key) = True
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                        
                    Loop
                Close #1
                
                cnt = 0
                For Each tmp In d.items
                    If tmp Then cnt = cnt + 1
                Next tmp
            End If
            
            If cnt <> d.Count Then
                MsgBox "QR入力ログが全て確認できませんでした｡"
            End If
            If cnt = d.Count Or flg Then
                MultiPage1.Pages(1).Enabled = True
                If cnt = d.Count Then Label_QRchk.Caption = "QR確認オッケー！"
            End If
            
        Else
            MsgBox "共有フォルダ保存用のCSVファイルを選択してください。"
        End If
    End If
End With


End Sub

Private Sub CmdBtn_CSV_Save_Click()

Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject
Dim f As file
Dim buf

Dim outFilePath As String
outFilePath = fso.BuildPath(TextBox_CSV_saveFolderPath, TextBox_CSV_Name.Value)

If fso.FileExists(outFilePath) Then
    If MsgBox("出力先でファイルが重複しています。" & vbCrLf & "上書きしてもよろしいでしょうか？", vbOKCancel) = vbCancel Then Exit Sub
End If


Dim cnt As Long
cnt = 1

Open outFilePath For Output As #2
    Open TextBox_CSV_SelectFilePath.Value For Input As #1
    
        Do Until EOF(1)
            Line Input #1, buf
            If cnt >= TextBox_CSV_StartLine.Value Then Print #2, buf
            cnt = cnt + 1
        Loop
    Close #1
Close #2

If fso.FileExists(outFilePath) Then
    MsgBox "保存に成功しました。"
Else
    MsgBox "保存先でファイルの作成に失敗しました。"
End If


End Sub

Sub updateTable_CycleTime_top1()
Dim tmp
Set tmp = Nothing
Dim flag As Boolean
Dim ws As Worksheet

If InStr(ActiveWorkbook.Name, "段取り予定") <> 0 Then
    For Each tmp In ActiveSheet.Parent.Sheets
        If tmp.Name = "サイクルタイム" Then
            Set ws = ActiveSheet.Parent.Sheets("サイクルタイム")
        End If
    Next tmp
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        ws.ListObjects("CycleTime_top1_").QueryTable.Refresh BackgroundQuery:=False
    End If
End If
End Sub

Private Sub CommandButton_CT_Select_Click()
Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp

Dim buf As String
Dim cnt As Long
cnt = 1
Dim flg As Boolean
flg = False

TextBox_CT_SelectPath.Value = "CSVファイルを選択"
TextBox_CT_JobName = ""
TextBox_CT_LineName = ""
TextBox_CT_SelectPath = ""

CommandButton_CT_Save.Enabled = False



With ListBox1
    If .ListIndex = -1 Then
        MsgBox "未選択です。"
    Else
        reg.Pattern = "^.*\.csv$"
        If reg.Test(.List(.ListIndex, 1)) Then
            Open .List(.ListIndex, 1) For Input As #1
                Do Until EOF(1)
                    Line Input #1, buf
                    
                    Select Case cnt
                        Case 1
                            reg.Pattern = "^生産データ: (.+)\.(prj|crb)$"
                            If reg.Test(buf) Then
                                TextBox_CT_JobName = reg.Execute(buf)(0).SubMatches(0)
                                TextBox_CT_LineName = Right(TextBox_CT_JobName, 1)
                            Else
                                flg = True
                            End If
                        Case 3
                            If buf <> "サイクルタイム" Then
                                flg = True
                            End If
                    End Select
                    
                    cnt = cnt + 1
                Loop
            Close #1
            
            If flg Then
                MsgBox "サイクルタイムが記述されているCSVを選択してください。"
            Else
                TextBox_CT_SelectPath.Value = .List(.ListIndex, 1)
                CommandButton_CT_Save.Enabled = True
            End If
        Else
            MsgBox "CSVファイルを選択してください。"
        End If
    End If
End With

End Sub

    Line Input #1, buf
    
    LineName = TextBox_CT_LineName
    
    NM = TextBox_CT_Author
    JobName = TextBox_CT_JobName
    Set rs = AdoCon.Execute("SELECT DISTINCT jobName FROM CycleTime_Points WHERE jobName ='" & JobName & "'")
    If rs.EOF = False Then
        MsgBox "すでに登録済みのJob名です｡"
        If MsgBox("同名Job名のレコードを全て削除して上書きしますか?", vbYesNo) = vbYes Then
            Call AdoCon.Execute("DELETE FROM CycleTime_Points WHERE jobName ='" & JobName & "'")
        Else
            MsgBox "処理を中止します｡"
            Close #1
            Exit Sub
        End If
    End If
    Set rs = Nothing
    
    
        Do Until buf = "サイクルタイム"
            Line Input #1, buf
            If EOF(1) Then
                MsgBox "サイクルタイムのデータが見つかりませんでした。"
                Exit Sub
            End If
        Loop
        cnt = 1
        Do Until cnt = 4
            Line Input #1, buf
            cnt = cnt + 1
        Loop
        cnt = 1
        Do
            Line Input #1, buf
            If buf = "" Then Exit Do
            
            Select Case cnt
            
            Case 1
                buf_split = Split(buf, ",")
                reg.Pattern = "^000(\d{9})_(.+)_(P\d+)$"
                If reg.Test(buf_split(0)) Then
                    myType.指図 = reg.Execute(buf_split(0))(0).SubMatches(0)
                    myType.品目 = reg.Execute(buf_split(0))(0).SubMatches.Item(1)
                    myType.印刷順序 = reg.Execute(buf_split(0))(0).SubMatches.Item(2)
                Else
                    Call MsgBox(buf_split(0) & vbCrLf & "↑読み取れませんでした。Skipします。", vbExclamation)
                    Line Input #1, buf
                    Line Input #1, buf
                    Line Input #1, buf
                    Line Input #1, buf
                    cnt = 0
                End If
    
                myType.サイクルタイム = buf_split(2)
            
            Case 2
                buf_split = Split(buf, ",")
                myType.総実装点 = buf_split(2)
            
            Case 3
                buf_split = Split(buf, ",")
                myType.ターン = buf_split(2)
            Case 4
                
            Case 5
                strSQL = "INSERT INTO CycleTime_Points(orderNumber, typeID, jobName, createrName, Pn, cycleTime, points, turn) "
                strSQL = strSQL + "VALUES('" & myType.指図 & "', '" & myType.品目 & "', '" & JobName & "', '" & TextBox_CT_Author.Text & "', '" & myType.印刷順序 & "', " & myType.サイクルタイム & ", " & myType.総実装点 & ", " & myType.ターン & ")"
                Call AdoCon.Execute(strSQL)
                
                DoEvents
                
                cnt = 0
                myType = myTypeDummy
            End Select
            
            cnt = cnt + 1
            
        Loop
    Close #1


AdoCon.Close
Set AdoCon = Nothing

Call updateTable_CycleTime_top1
MsgBox "完了"

MultiPage1.Pages(2).Enabled = True

Exit Sub

e:
MsgBox err.Description

End Sub

Private Sub TextBox_CT_Author_Change()
TextBox_Job_TantoName.Value = TextBox_CT_Author.Value
End Sub

            CommandButton_crb_Save.Enabled = True

            CommandButton_crb_Save.Enabled = False
        End If
    End If

Private Sub CommandButton_crb_save_Click()

On Error GoTo err


'Dim STRM As Object
'Set STRM = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
Dim STRM As ADODB.Stream
Set STRM = New ADODB.Stream
Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject
Dim buf As String, target As String

Dim TMP_Folder As String
Dim TMP_crb As String

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
Dim reg2 As RegExp
Set reg2 = New RegExp
reg.Pattern = "^\[LotNames]$"
reg2.Pattern = "^\[PositionData<(\d+)>\]$"


target = TextBox_crb_SelectPath.Value


TMP_Folder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(TemporaryFolder)
TMP_crb = fso.BuildPath(TMP_Folder, "crb.txt")

Dim JobName As String
JobName = fso.GetBaseName(target)

'ADODB.Streamさんは未知の拡張子だとエラー返すようなのでユーザーディレクトリのtmpフォルダへコピーしてリネーム
fso.CopyFile target, TMP_crb


Dim PCB_Name As String
ReDim orderNumbers(0) As orderNumber
Dim num As Long
Dim Symbol_Name As String
Dim d As Dictionary
Set d = New Dictionary
Dim Lot As String

Dim D_Err As Dictionary
Set D_Err = New Dictionary

With STRM
.Charset = "UTF-8"
.Open
.LoadFromFile TMP_crb

'最終行まで
Do Until .EOS
    
    '1行進める
    buf = .ReadText(-2)
    
    '[LotNames]
    If reg.Test(buf) Then
        .ReadText (-2)
        reg.Pattern = "^""000(\d{9})_(.+)_(P\d+)""$"
        buf = .ReadText(-2)
        'LOTNUM順にorderNumberオブジェクト生成､指図を入れる
        Do Until buf = ""
            Lot = Split(buf, " ")(2)
            num = CLng(Split(buf, " ")(1) - 1)
            If reg.Test(Lot) Then
                If UBound(orderNumbers) < num Then
                    ReDim Preserve orderNumbers(num)
                End If
                Set orderNumbers(num) = New orderNumber
                orderNumbers(num).Name = reg.Execute(Lot)(0).SubMatches(0)
                orderNumbers(num).JobName = JobName
                orderNumbers(num).TypeID = reg.Execute(Lot)(0).SubMatches(1)
                orderNumbers(num).Pn = reg.Execute(Lot)(0).SubMatches(2)
                orderNumbers(num).ver = Replace(Split(buf, " ")(11), """", "")
            Else
                'MsgBox Lot + vbCrLf + "Err"
                If Not D_Err.Exists(num) Then D_Err.add num, True
                'Stop
            End If
            buf = .ReadText(-2)
        Loop
    End If
    
    '[PositionData<[LOTNUM]>]
    If reg2.Test(buf) Then
        '対応するLOTNUMを格納
        num = CLng(reg2.Execute(buf)(0).SubMatches(0)) - 1
        .ReadText (-2)
        buf = .ReadText(-2)
        '空白行まで読み続ける
        Do Until buf = ""
            '対応するLOTNUMのorderNumber.Dへ格納 key=シンボル　item=PARTSID
            Symbol_Name = CStr(Replace(Split(buf, " ")(24), """", ""))
            If Symbol_Name <> "SIKAKU" And Symbol_Name <> "" And D_Err(num) = "" Then
                If orderNumbers(num).d.Exists(Symbol_Name) Then
                    If orderNumbers(num).d(Symbol_Name) <> Split(buf, " ")(5) Then
                        MsgBox "Err"
                        Stop
                    End If
                Else
                    orderNumbers(num).d.add Symbol_Name, Split(buf, " ")(5)
                End If
            End If
            buf = .ReadText(-2)
            If Label_crb.Caption <> "シンボル読み取り中" Then
                Label_crb.Caption = "シンボル読み取り中"
                DoEvents
            End If
        Loop
    End If
    
    'PartsDataの辞書作成 key=PARTSID key=部品コード
    If buf = "[PartsData]" Then
        .ReadText (-2)
        buf = .ReadText(-2)
        Do Until buf = ""
            d.add Split(buf, " ")(0), CStr(Replace(Split(buf, " ")(1), """", ""))
            buf = .ReadText(-2)
            If Label_crb.Caption <> "部品読み取り中" Then
                Label_crb.Caption = "部品読み取り中"
                DoEvents
            End If
        Loop
    End If
Loop
.Close
End With





Dim tmp
Dim i As Long
Dim strSQL As String

Dim AdoCon As ADODB.Connection
Set AdoCon = New ADODB.Connection
AdoCon.CursorLocation = adUseServer ' クライアントサイドカーソルに変更
AdoCon.Open "Driver={MySQL ODBC 8.0 Unicode Driver};Server=nksv1359.ad.nkc.co.jp;Database=QCD;User=2prod1-admin;Password=SMTjissoadmin;"

Dim rs As Recordset
Set rs = New Recordset

Dim First_Flag As Boolean
Dim Skip_Flag As Boolean
Dim SecondOrderNumber_flag As Boolean

Dim D2 As Dictionary
Set D2 = New Dictionary

For i = 0 To UBound(orderNumbers)
    DoEvents
    Label_crb.Caption = "データ送信中" & i & "/" & (UBound(orderNumbers) + 1)
    DoEvents
    
    If Not D_Err(i) Then
    
        If Not D2.Exists(orderNumbers(i).Name) Then
            D2.add orderNumbers(i).Name, orderNumbers(i).Name
            SecondOrderNumber_flag = False
        Else
            SecondOrderNumber_flag = True
        End If
        
        Skip_Flag = False
        
        Set rs = AdoCon.Execute("SELECT DISTINCT orderNumber FROM orderNumberBOM WHERE orderNumber=""" + orderNumbers(i).Name + """;")
        If rs.EOF = False And SecondOrderNumber_flag = False Then
            If MsgBox("指図が重複しました｡[" + orderNumbers(i).Name + "]" + vbCrLf + "既存の指図のレコードを全て削除して上書きしますか?", vbYesNo) = vbYes Then
                Call AdoCon.Execute("DELETE FROM orderNumberBOM WHERE orderNumber ='" & orderNumbers(i).Name & "'")
                Call AdoCon.Execute("UPDATE orderNumberBOM_Log SET Deleted = 1 WHERE orderNumber ='" & orderNumbers(i).Name & "';")
            Else
                If MsgBox("飛ばして処理を続行しますか?", vbOKCancel) = vbOK Then
                    Skip_Flag = True
                Else
                    MsgBox "処理を中止します｡"
                    Exit Sub
                End If
            End If
        End If
        
        
        If Skip_Flag = False Then
            First_Flag = True
            For Each tmp In orderNumbers(i).d
                
                If First_Flag Then
                    strSQL = "INSERT INTO orderNumberBOM(orderNumber, Symbol, PartNo)  VALUES"
                    strSQL = strSQL + "(""" + orderNumbers(i).Name + """, """ + tmp + """, """ + d(orderNumbers(i).d(tmp)) + """)"
                    First_Flag = False
                Else
                    strSQL = strSQL + ",(""" + orderNumbers(i).Name + """, """ + tmp + """, """ + d(orderNumbers(i).d(tmp)) + """ )"
                End If
                DoEvents
            Next tmp
            Call AdoCon.Execute(strSQL)
            
            strSQL = "INSERT INTO orderNumberBOM_Log(orderNumber, JobName, TypeID, Pn, Ver) VALUES("
            strSQL = strSQL + "'" + orderNumbers(i).Name + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).JobName + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).TypeID + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).Pn + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).ver + "');"
            
            Call AdoCon.Execute(strSQL)
            
        End If
    End If
Next i

AdoCon.Close
Set AdoCon = Nothing


Set tmp = Nothing
For Each tmp In MultiPage1.Pages
    tmp.Enabled = True
Next tmp


Label_crb.Caption = ""
MsgBox "OK"

Exit Sub

err:
MsgBox "エラー"
MsgBox err.Description


End Sub

Sub setDictionary_kumitori_daisu(ByRef D_Kumitori As Dictionary, ByRef D_Daisu As Dictionary)

Dim con As ADODB.Connection
Set con = New ADODB.Connection

con.ConnectionString = "Driver={MySQL ODBC 8.0 Unicode Driver};" & _
                      "Server=nksv1359.ad.nkc.co.jp;" & _
                      "Database=scheduled;" & _
                      "User=2prod1-user;" & _
                      "Password=SMTjisso"
con.Open

Dim rs As ADODB.Recordset
Set rs = New ADODB.Recordset

rs.Open "SELECT `typeID`, `chldID` FROM `unitInfo` WHERE `chldID` LIKE "",%"" AND `chldID` NOT LIKE "",%,%"";", con

'組取り数辞書作成
Do Until rs.EOF
    D_Kumitori.add CStr(rs("typeID")), CInt(Replace(rs("chldID"), ",", ""))
    rs.MoveNext
Loop

rs.Close

rs.Open "SELECT `orderNumber`, `orderQty` FROM `liveOrder`", con

'台数辞書作成
Do Until rs.EOF
    D_Daisu.add CStr(rs("orderNumber")), CInt(rs("orderQty"))
    rs.MoveNext
Loop

rs.Close: Set rs = Nothing
con.Close: Set con = Nothing

End Sub

Sub Tray(ByRef ws As Worksheet)

Dim i As Long
Dim Tgt As Range
Dim tmp As Range
Dim a As Long
Dim flag As Boolean

For i = 1 To ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    If ws.Cells(i, 1).Value = "フィーダーセットアップ" Then
        Range(ws.Rows(2), ws.Rows(i)).Delete
        flag = True
        Exit For
    End If
Next i

If flag = False Then
    MsgBox """フィーダーセットアップ""行が見つかりません。"
    Exit Sub
End If


For i = 9 To ws.Cells(2, Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column
    '部品説明まで来たらやめる
    If ws.Cells(2, i).Value = "部品説明" Then Exit For
    
    '計算する範囲
    Set Tgt = Range(ws.Cells(3, i), ws.Cells(Rows.Count, i).End(xlUp))
    '合計値
    a = Application.WorksheetFunction.Sum(Tgt)
    'もし0なら
    If a = 0 Then
        '消すリストに追加
        If tmp Is Nothing Then
            Set tmp = Columns(i)
        Else
            Set tmp = Union(tmp, Columns(i))
        End If
    End If
    
Next i

If Not tmp Is Nothing Then tmp.Delete

End Sub


Private Sub CmdBtn_Tray_Select_Click()

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
reg.Pattern = "^.*\.csv$"
Dim i As Long
Dim tmp

Dim D_Kumitori As Dictionary
Set D_Kumitori = New Dictionary
Dim D_Daisu As Dictionary
Set D_Daisu = New Dictionary

On Error Resume Next
Call setDictionary_kumitori_daisu(D_Kumitori, D_Daisu)
On Error GoTo 0

With ListBox1
    If .ListIndex = -1 Then
        MsgBox "未選択です。"
    Else
        If reg.Test(.List(.ListIndex, 1)) Then
            Application.ScreenUpdating = False
            With Workbooks.Open(.List(.ListIndex, 1))
                Call Tray(.Sheets(1))
                ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = False
                .Sheets(1).Cells.Copy Sheet_Tray2.Cells
                ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = True
                Application.ScreenUpdating = True
                .Close False
            End With
            
            Set d = New Dictionary
            ListBox_T.Clear
            ListBox_T.ColumnWidths = "40;300"
            ListBox_T.ColumnCount = 2
            
            reg.Pattern = "^000(\d{9})_(.+)_P\d+$"
            
            For i = 9 To Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, Sheet_Tray2.Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column
                '部品説明まで来たらやめる
                If Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value = "部品説明" Then Exit For
                tmp = Range(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i), Sheet_Tray2.Cells(Sheet_Tray2.Rows.Count, i).End(xlUp))
                If reg.Test(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value) Then
                    If D_Daisu.Exists(CStr(reg.Execute(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value)(0).SubMatches(0))) And _
                       D_Kumitori.Exists(CStr(reg.Execute(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value)(0).SubMatches(1))) Then
                        Call d.add(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value, Array(tmp, D_Daisu(CStr(reg.Execute(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value)(0).SubMatches(0))), D_Kumitori(CStr(reg.Execute(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value)(0).SubMatches(1)))))
                        Call ListBox_T.AddItem(D_Daisu(CStr(reg.Execute(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value)(0).SubMatches(0))) & ", " & D_Kumitori(CStr(reg.Execute(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value)(0).SubMatches(1))))
                    Else
                        Call d.add(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value, Array(tmp, 0, 0))
                        Call ListBox_T.AddItem("未")
                    End If
                Else
                    Call d.add(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value, Array(tmp, 0, 0))
                    Call ListBox_T.AddItem("未")
                End If
                                
                ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListCount - 1, 1) = Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, i).Value
            Next i
            
            If ListBox_T.ListCount <> 0 Then
                ListBox_T.ListIndex = 0
            Else
                OptionButton_Job_1.Value = 1
            End If
        Else
            MsgBox "CSVファイルを選択してください。"
        End If
    End If
End With

End Sub

Private Sub CmdBtn_Tray_UP_Click()
Dim tmp As String
Dim tmp2 As String
Dim n As Long
With ListBox_T
n = .ListIndex
If n > 0 Then
    tmp = .List(n, 0)
    tmp2 = .List(n, 1)
    Call .RemoveItem(n)
    Call .AddItem(tmp, n - 1)
    .List((n - 1), 1) = tmp2
    .ListIndex = n - 1
End If
End With
End Sub


Private Sub CmdBtn_Tray_DOWN_Click()
Dim tmp As String
Dim tmp2 As String
Dim n As Long
With ListBox_T
n = .ListIndex
If n <> .ListCount - 1 And n <> -1 Then
    tmp = .List(n, 0)
    tmp2 = .List(n, 1)
    Call .RemoveItem(n)
    Call .AddItem(tmp, n + 1)
    .List((n + 1), 1) = tmp2
    .ListIndex = n + 1
End If
End With
End Sub

Private Sub ListBox_T_Change()
If ListBox_T.ListIndex = -1 Then Exit Sub
If IsNull(ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1)) Then Exit Sub

TextBox_Tray_daisu.Value = d(ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1))(1)
TextBox_Tray_mensu.Value = d(ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1))(2)

End Sub

Private Sub TextBox_Tray_daisu_Change()
If ListBox_T.ListIndex = -1 Or TextBox_Tray_daisu.Value = "" Then Exit Sub

If Not IsNumeric(TextBox_Tray_daisu.Value) Then
    MsgBox "数値以外は入力できません。"
    TextBox_Tray_daisu.Value = 0
    Exit Sub
End If

Dim key As String
key = ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1)
d(key) = Array(d(key)(0), TextBox_Tray_daisu.Value, d(key)(2))
Call Textbox_Tray_Change
End Sub

Private Sub TextBox_Tray_mensu_Change()
If ListBox_T.ListIndex = -1 Or TextBox_Tray_mensu.Value = "" Then Exit Sub

If Not IsNumeric(TextBox_Tray_mensu.Value) Then
    MsgBox "数値以外は入力できません。"
    TextBox_Tray_mensu.Value = 0
    Exit Sub
End If

Dim key As String
key = ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1)
d(key) = Array(d(key)(0), d(key)(1), TextBox_Tray_mensu.Value)
Call Textbox_Tray_Change
End Sub

Private Sub Textbox_Tray_Change()
If d(ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1))(1) > 0 And d(ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1))(2) > 0 Then
    ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 0) = d(ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1))(1) & ", " & d(ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 1))(2)
Else
    ListBox_T.List(ListBox_T.ListIndex, 0) = "未"
End If

Dim cnt As Long
Dim i As Long
cnt = 0

For i = 0 To ListBox_T.ListCount - 1
    If ListBox_T.List(i, 0) = "未" Then cnt = cnt + 1
Next i

If cnt = 0 Then
    CmdBtn_Tray_Create.Enabled = True
Else
    CmdBtn_Tray_Create.Enabled = False
End If
End Sub


Private Sub CmdBtn_Tray_Create_Click()

Dim i As Long
Dim col_cnt As Long
col_cnt = 9
Dim key As String

Application.Dialogs(xlDialogPrinterSetup).Show

For i = 0 To ListBox_T.ListCount - 1
    key = ListBox_T.List(i, 1)
    Range(Sheet_Tray2.Cells(2, col_cnt), Sheet_Tray2.Cells(1 + UBound(d(key)(0), 1), col_cnt)) = d(key)(0)
    Sheet_Tray3.Cells(3, col_cnt).Value = d(key)(1)
    Sheet_Tray3.Cells(4, col_cnt).Value = d(key)(2)
    col_cnt = col_cnt + 1
Next i

Application.ScreenUpdating = False
On Error GoTo Cleanup

With Workbooks.add
    ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = False

    Dim tray1bHasParts As Boolean
    tray1bHasParts = (WorksheetFunction.CountBlank(Sheet_Tray1b.Range(Sheet_Tray1b.Cells(5, 3), Sheet_Tray1b.Cells(24, 3))) <> 20)

    Dim srcSheets As Variant
    srcSheets = Array(Sheet_Tray1b, Sheet_Tray1a)

    Dim numCols As Long
    numCols = i + 6

    Dim wsSrc As Variant
    Dim copied As Worksheet

    For Each wsSrc In srcSheets
        ' Sheet_Tray1b の指定範囲がすべて空ならスキップ
        If wsSrc Is Sheet_Tray1b And Not tray1bHasParts Then
            ' スキップ
        Else
            ' シートを新規ブックの先頭にコピー
            wsSrc.Copy Before:=.Sheets(1)
            Set copied = .Sheets(1)
    
            ' PrintArea を列幅に合わせて設定
            On Error Resume Next
            If Application.ReferenceStyle <> xlA1 Then
                Application.ReferenceStyle = xlA1
                copied.PageSetup.PrintArea = copied.Range(copied.PageSetup.PrintArea).Resize(, numCols).Address(ReferenceStyle:=xlA1)
                Application.ReferenceStyle = xlR1C1
            Else
                copied.PageSetup.PrintArea = copied.Range(copied.PageSetup.PrintArea).Resize(, numCols).Address(ReferenceStyle:=xlA1)
            End If
            On Error GoTo 0
    
            With copied
                ' 既存の改ページをクリア
                .ResetAllPageBreaks
    
                ' 印刷範囲全体を1ページ幅に収める
                With .PageSetup
                    .Zoom = False
                    .FitToPagesWide = 1
                    .FitToPagesTall = 1
                End With
    
                ' 条件により「'1/2」を挿入
                If wsSrc Is Sheet_Tray1a And tray1bHasParts Then
                    .Cells(2, 1).Value = "'1/2"
                    OptionButton_Job_3.Value = 1
                End If
    
                ' 印刷
                .PrintOut From:=1, To:=1
            End With
        End If
    Next wsSrc

    .Close SaveChanges:=False

    ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = True
End With

Cleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub


Private Sub TextBox_Tray_mensu_KeyDown(ByVal KeyCode As MSForms.ReturnInteger, ByVal Shift As Integer)
If KeyCode = vbKeyReturn Then Call Tray_Next
End Sub

Private Sub Tray_Next()

If ListBox_T.ListIndex <> ListBox_T.ListCount - 1 And ListBox_T.ListIndex <> -1 Then
    ListBox_T.ListIndex = ListBox_T.ListIndex + 1
    TextBox_Tray_daisu.SetFocus
    TextBox_Tray_daisu.SelStart = 0
    TextBox_Tray_daisu.SelLength = Len(TextBox_Tray_daisu.Text)
End If
End Sub

Private Sub CmdBtn_Job_BaseCreate_Click()

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
reg.Multiline = False
Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject
Dim File_Jissoujokyou As file
Set File_Jissoujokyou = fso.GetFile(TextBox_Job_JissoujokyouFolderPath)


Dim JobTime As Double
JobTime = 0
Dim sel As Range
Dim qty As Range
Dim lastOrder As Range
Dim j As Long

'自動入力機能　表の範囲決定
If CheckBox_Job_Auto Then
    'reg.Pattern = "^[0-9A-Z]+-[0-9A-Z]+$"
    'If Not reg.test(Selection.Text) Then
    If TextBox_JobName.Text <> Selection.Text Then
        MsgBox ("自動入力機能を使用するには､段取り予定ExcelのJob名部分を選択してください｡")
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If TextBox_JobName.Text = "" Then
        MsgBox ("自動入力機能を使用するには､Job名を入力してください｡")
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    reg.Pattern = "^\d{9}$"
    j = 1
    '指図が無くなる行まで
    Do While (reg.Test(Selection.Offset(j, 3).Value))
        If Selection.Column = 14 And Not IsError(Selection.Offset(j, -10).Value) Then JobTime = JobTime + Selection.Offset(j, -10).Value
        j = j + 1
    Loop
    j = j - 1
    Set qty = Range(Selection.Offset(1, -2), Selection.Offset(j, -2)) ' 段取り予定からシート数の列を取得
    Set sel = Range(Selection.Offset(1, -1), Selection.Offset(j, 10)) ' 段取り予定から「温プロ基板」以降の列を取得
End If


Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

Dim i As Long
Dim n As String
Dim m As Long
Dim tmp
Dim flg
For Each tmp In Workbooks
    If tmp.Name = "実装状況表2.xlsx" Then flg = True
Next tmp

If Not flg Then
    If MsgBox("高速化の為実装状況表を開きます｡", vbYesNo) = vbYes Then
        Workbooks.Open "\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\SMTData\実装状況表2.xlsx"
        Application.WindowState = xlNormal
        Application.Top = 0
        Application.WindowState = xlMaximized
        flg = True
    End If
End If

With Workbooks.add
    ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = False
    ' TOOL.xlamからテンプレートをコピー
    Sheet_ZissouShiji.Copy .Sheets(1)
    Sheet_Job.Copy .Sheets(1)
    ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = True
    
    With .Sheets(1) ' 実装状況表貼り付け用
        
        .Cells(5, 6).Value = TextBox_JobName.Text
        .Cells(5, 8).Value = "作成者 " & TextBox_Job_TantoName
        If OptionButton_Job_1 Then
            .Cells(5, 4).Value = "無し"
            .Cells(5, 4).Interior.Color = 65535
        ElseIf OptionButton_Job_2 Then
            .Cells(5, 4).Value = "Aのみ"
        Else
            .Cells(5, 4).Value = "A/B"
        End If
        
        .Range(.Cells(6, 1), .Cells(50, 1)).FormulaR1C1 = _
                "=VLOOKUP(RC[7],'" & File_Jissoujokyou.ParentFolder & "\[" & File_Jissoujokyou.Name & "]温度プロファイル'!C1:C2,2,FALSE)"
        ' .Range(.Cells(6, 2), .Cells(50, 2)).FormulaR1C1 = _
        '        "=RC[8]/VLOOKUP(RC[6],'" & File_Jissoujokyou.ParentFolder & "\[" & File_Jissoujokyou.Name & "]シート数'!C1:C2,2,FALSE)"
        
        '自動入力機能
        If CheckBox_Job_Auto Then
            qty.Copy ' 段取り予定からシート数をコピー
            .Cells(6, 2).PasteSpecial Paste:=xlPasteValues
            sel.Copy
            .Cells(6, 5).PasteSpecial Paste:=xlPasteValues
            Set lastOrder = .Cells(.Rows.Count, 9).End(xlUp)
            
            For i = 6 To lastOrder.row
                ' 試作と初ロットに書式適用
                If .Cells(i, 5).Value Like "試作" Then
                    .Range("A60", "P60").Copy
                    .Cells(i, 1).PasteSpecial Paste:=xlPasteFormats
                End If
                If .Cells(i, 6).Value Like "*初ロット*" Then
                    .Range("A61", "P61").Copy
                    .Cells(i, 1).PasteSpecial Paste:=xlPasteFormats
                End If
            Next i
            ' 不要行を削除
            Range(lastOrder.EntireRow.Offset(1, 0), .Rows(.Rows.Count)).Delete
            
            If JobTime <> 0 Then
                lastOrder.Offset(2, -3).Value = "実装時間:" & Int(JobTime) & "時間" & Round((JobTime - Int(JobTime)) * 60, 0) & "分"
            End If
            ' 試作が含まれる場合は実装時間に「+試作」を追記
            If Application.WorksheetFunction.CountIf(Range("E6", "E" & CStr(lastOrder.row)), "試作") <> 0 Then
                lastOrder.Offset(2, -3).Value = lastOrder.Offset(2, -3).Value & " + 試作"
            End If
            
            For i = 6 To lastOrder.row
                ' 鉛フリーなら背景を薄赤に
                If InStr(.Cells(i, 13).Value, "鉛フリー") <> 0 Then
                    .Cells(i, 13).Interior.Color = 9737946
                End If
                ' bolden関数で対象セル内の指定文字列または指定文字列以降をボールド体にする
                bolden .Cells(i, 13), 0, "マガジンラックピッチ""3"""
                bolden .Cells(i, 6), 1, "作業連絡"
            Next i
            ' 行の高さを自動調整し、高さが27未満になったら27に戻す
            .Rows(6 & ":" & lastOrder.row).EntireRow.AutoFit
            For i = 6 To lastOrder.row
                If .Rows(i).RowHeight < 27 Then .Rows(i).RowHeight = 27
            Next i
        End If
        
        
        Dim wsName As String
        Dim ws As Worksheet
        If flg Then
            ' 実装状況表が開いていたら、貼り付け先候補セルをSelectする
            n = Right(.Cells(5, 6).Value, 1)
            If IsNumeric(Mid(.Cells(5, 6).Value, 9, 1)) Then
                m = Mid(.Cells(5, 6).Value, 9, 1)
                If m <> 0 Then
                    m = 1
                End If
            Else
                m = 0
            End If
            wsName = "実装日程　" & n & "ライン"
            For Each ws In Workbooks("実装状況表2.xlsx").Worksheets
                If Trim$(ws.Name) = wsName Then '2ラインだけ何故か半角スペース入っているのでTrim
                    ws.Activate
                    If IsNumeric(m) Then
                        ws.Cells(10000, 1).End(xlUp).Offset(6 - m, 0).Select
                    End If
                End If
            Next ws
        End If
        ' コピー範囲をSelectする
        .Activate
        If Not lastOrder Is Nothing Then
            .Rows("3:" & lastOrder.row + 2).Select
        Else
            .Activate
        End If
    End With
    
    With .Sheets(2) ' 実装指示印刷用シート
        .Cells(3, 3).Formula = "=job!F5"
        .Range(.Cells(4, 1), .Cells(100, 1)).FormulaR1C1 = _
                "=VLOOKUP(RC[4],'" & File_Jissoujokyou.ParentFolder & "\[" & File_Jissoujokyou.Name & "]温度プロファイル'!C1:C2,2,FALSE)"
        .Range(.Cells(4, 2), .Cells(100, 2)).FormulaR1C1 = _
                 "=INDEX(job!C2:C9, MATCH(RC6, job!C9:C9, 0), 1)" ' 実装状況表貼り付け用のjobシートから指図でシート数を検索
                ' "=RC[5]/VLOOKUP(RC[3],'" & File_Jissoujokyou.ParentFolder & "\[" & File_Jissoujokyou.Name & "]シート数'!C1:C2,2,FALSE)"
        If CheckBox_Job_Auto Then
            .Activate
            Call JissouShiji
        End If
    End With
    
    With .Sheets(1)
        'ノズル･フィーダー追加指示
        If CheckBox_Job_Nozzle Then
            .Cells(5, 9).Value = "実装開始前 T*-* ****ノズル[追加/交換]。　実装完了後[装着継続/取り外して下さい。]"
        End If
        If CheckBox_Job_Feeder Then
            If .Cells(5, 9).Value = "" Then
                .Cells(5, 9).Value = "実装開始前 T*-* 部品:****[追加/交換/外し]してください｡"
            Else
                .Rows(6).Insert
                .Cells(6, 9).Value = "実装開始前 T*-* 部品:****[追加/交換/外し]してください｡"
            End If
        End If
    End With
    
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    .Sheets(3).Delete
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    .Activate
    With Application
        .WindowState = xlNormal
        .Top = (GetSystemMetrics(1) / 4) - 20
        .Width = GetSystemMetrics(0) / 2
        .Height = GetSystemMetrics(1) / 2
    End With
    
End With

End Sub

' 0:キーワード単体　1:キーワード以降の文字列
Private Sub bolden(ByVal dstCell As Range, ByVal mode As Long, ByVal key As String)
    Dim txt As String
    Dim pos As Long
    
    ' --- ガード ---
    If dstCell Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If dstCell.HasArray Then Exit Sub
    If dstCell.HasFormula Then Exit Sub           ' 数式セルは部分書式不可
    If IsError(dstCell.Value) Then Exit Sub
    
    txt = CStr(dstCell.Value2)
    If Len(txt) = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Select Case mode
        Case 0
            ' -------- ① リテラル一致（最初の1箇所だけ） --------
            pos = InStr(1, txt, key, vbTextCompare)
            If pos > 0 Then
                dstCell.Characters(pos, Len(key)).Font.Bold = True
            End If
        Case 1
            ' -------- ② キーワード以降～末尾まで --------
            ' 改行（vbLf）を含んでいてもOK。InStrで位置を取得して末尾までBoldにする
            pos = InStr(1, txt, key, vbTextCompare)
            If pos > 0 Then
                dstCell.Characters(pos, Len(txt) - pos + 1).Font.Bold = True
            End If
        Case Else
            ' 何もしない
    End Select
End Sub

VBA MACRO Module4.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/Module4'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit


Sub Utf8ToSjis(a_sFrom, a_sTo)
    Dim streamRead  As New ADODB.Stream '// 読み込みデータ
    Dim streamWrite As New ADODB.Stream '// 書き込みデータ
    Dim sText                           '// ファイルデータ
    
    '// ファイル読み込み
    streamRead.Type = adTypeText
    streamRead.Charset = "UTF-8"
    streamRead.Open
    Call streamRead.LoadFromFile(a_sFrom)
    
    '// 改行コードLFをCRLFに変換
    sText = streamRead.ReadText
    sText = Replace(sText, vbLf, vbCrLf)
    sText = Replace(sText, vbCr & vbCr, vbCr)
    
    '// ファイル書き込み
    streamWrite.Type = adTypeText
    streamWrite.Charset = "Shift-JIS"
    streamWrite.Open
    
    '// データ書き込み
    Call streamWrite.WriteText(sText)
    
    '// 保存
    Call streamWrite.SaveToFile(a_sTo, adSaveCreateOverWrite)
    
    '// クローズ
    streamRead.Close
    streamWrite.Close
End Sub



Sub MBOM変換()
Dim fso As New FileSystemObject
Dim FD As Folder
Dim f As file

Dim FolderName As String

With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFolderPicker)
    .AllowMultiSelect = False
    If .Show = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not fso.FolderExists(.SelectedItems(1)) Then
        Call MsgBox("指定フォルダが見つかりません。", vbCritical)
        Exit Sub
    Else
        Set FD = fso.GetFolder(.SelectedItems(1))
    End If
End With


Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
reg.Pattern = "^.+_((.+)-A\.csv)$"
reg.Global = True
reg.Multiline = False

For Each f In FD.Files
    FolderName = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(1)
    If Not fso.FolderExists(FD.Path & "\" & FolderName) Then
        fso.CreateFolder (FD.Path & "\" & FolderName)
    End If
    Call Utf8ToSjis(f.Path, FD.Path & "\" & FolderName & "\M-BOM_" & reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(0))
Next f


End Sub



Sub namaekaeru()

Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject
Dim FD As Folder
Dim f As file

Set FD = fso.GetFolder("\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\鹿島エレクトロニクス進捗\データ保管場所\20210827")

For Each FD In FD.SubFolders
    For Each f In FD.Files
        
        If fso.GetExtensionName(f.Name) = "csv" Then
            f.Name = "M-BOM_" & f.Name
        End If
        
    Next f
Next FD

End Sub


Sub PCB()
Dim fso As New FileSystemObject
Dim FD As Folder
Dim f As file

Dim FolderName As String

With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFolderPicker)
    .AllowMultiSelect = False
    If .Show = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not fso.FolderExists(.SelectedItems(1)) Then
        Call MsgBox("指定フォルダが見つかりません。", vbCritical)
        Exit Sub
    Else
        Set FD = fso.GetFolder(.SelectedItems(1))
    End If
End With

Dim reg As New RegExp
reg.Pattern = "^N?(([A-Z]+)([\d-]+)_P.+)$"
reg.Global = True
reg.Multiline = False

Dim HinmokuName As String

For Each f In FD.Files
    If reg.Test(f.Name) = False Then Stop
    
    HinmokuName = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(1) & "S-" & reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(2)
    
    If Not fso.FolderExists(FD.Path & "\" & HinmokuName) Then
        Call fso.CreateFolder(FD.Path & "\" & HinmokuName)
    End If
    
    Call f.Copy(FD.Path & "\" & HinmokuName & "\" & reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(0))
    
    
Next f


End Sub


Sub x3ライン看板用データ()

Dim i As Long
Dim r As Long
Dim c As Long
Dim A3 As Worksheet
Set A3 = Sheets("出力")
Dim Dt As Worksheet
Set Dt = Sheets("データ")

i = 2

Dim cnt As Long
cnt = 0

Application.Dialogs(xlDialogPrinterSetup).Show

If MsgBox("開始します", vbYesNo) = vbNo Then Exit Sub

Do While i <= Dt.Cells(Dt.Rows.Count, 6).End(xlUp).row
    
    For r = 2 To 18 Step 4
        For c = 2 To 15 Step 4
        If Dt.Cells(i, 6).Value <> "" Then
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(0, 1).Value = Dt.Cells(i, 16).Value
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(0, 3).Value = Dt.Cells(i, 15).Value
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(1, 1).Value = Dt.Cells(i, 6).Value
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(2, 0).Value = "*" & Dt.Cells(i, 6).Value & "*"
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(3, 0).Value = Dt.Cells(i, 14).Value
            
        Else
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(1, 1).Value = ""
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(2, 0).Value = ""
            A3.Cells(r, c).Offset(3, 0).Value = ""
        End If
        cnt = cnt + 1
        If cnt = Dt.Cells(i, 13) Then
            cnt = 0
            i = i + 1
        End If
        Next c
        
    Next r
    
    A3.PrintOut

Loop


End Sub

VBA MACRO Item.cls 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/Item'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit

Public speed As String
Public print1 As String
Public print2 As String
Public HDP As String
Public BHDP As String
Public Hinmoku_trim
Private cel_() As Variant

Public Function RYOMEN() As Boolean
If print2 = "" Then
    RYOMEN = False
Else
    RYOMEN = True
End If
End Function

Public Function KOBETU() As Boolean
If InStr(speed, "個別") <> 0 Or InStr(speed, "特殊") <> 0 Then
    KOBETU = True
Else
    KOBETU = False
End If
End Function

Property Let Cel(Cel As Variant)
    cel_ = Cel
End Property
Property Get Cel() As Variant
    Cel = cel_
End Property


Property Let hinmoku(hinmoku As String)
    Dim reg As RegExp
    Set reg = New RegExp
    reg.Pattern = "^(.+?)S?(-\d{4}).*$"
    
    If reg.Test(hinmoku) Then
        Hinmoku_trim = reg.Execute(hinmoku)(0).SubMatches(0) + reg.Execute(hinmoku)(0).SubMatches(1)
    Else
        Hinmoku_trim = hinmoku
    End If
    
End Property

Property Get hinmoku() As String

    hinmoku = Hinmoku_trim

End Property

Sub insertSUUSHIKI_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)

    MsgBox "対応外のシート"
    End
End If

Application.Calculation = xlManual

    If Cells(i, 17).Value <> "" Then
        If tmp Is Nothing Then
            Set tmp = Range(Cells(i, 1), Cells(i, 12))
        Else
            Set tmp = Union(tmp, Range(Cells(i, 1), Cells(i, 12)))
        End If
    End If
    
    
Next i

Sub KakoJob_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
Dim s As Long
Dim e As Long
Dim ToSheet As String
s = Selection.row
e = Selection.row + 1
ToSheet = "L" & Right(Selection.Value, 1)

reg.Pattern = "^\d{6}-\w+$"


If ActiveSheet.Name <> "job作成" Then
    MsgBox "対応外のシート"
    End
End If

Dim tmp

For Each tmp In Selection.Areas

    If tmp.Count = 1 Then
        
        If Not reg.Test(tmp.Value) Then
            MsgBox "Job名がない部分が選択されています｡"
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        s = tmp.row
        e = tmp.row + 1
        ToSheet = "L" & Right(tmp.Value, 1)

        If Selection.Column <> 2 Then
            MsgBox "Job名が2列目にありません｡" & vbCrLf & "※左に数式が入っている場合､削除してから実行してください｡"
            Exit Sub
        End If

        Do Until Cells(e, 5).Value = ""
            e = e + 1
        Loop
        e = e - 1

        Range(Rows(s), Rows(e)).Cut Sheets(ToSheet).UsedRange.Rows(Sheets(ToSheet).UsedRange.Rows.Count).Offset(1, 0)

    End If
Next tmp



End Sub

Sub egprobom()

Dim CurrentSheet As Worksheet
Set CurrentSheet = ActiveSheet

Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject

Dim f As file
Dim TargetRow As Long

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
reg.Pattern = "^(.+)_.+-A\.csv$"

For Each f In fso.GetFolder("\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\RPA\ロボット(本番)\部品表ダウンロード\DL").Files
    
    If reg.Test(f.Name) Then
        
        With Workbooks.Open(f.Path)
            With .Sheets(1)
                TargetRow = CurrentSheet.UsedRange(CurrentSheet.UsedRange.Count).row + 1
                Range(.Cells(3, 2), .Cells(.Rows.Count, 2).End(xlUp)).Copy CurrentSheet.Cells(TargetRow, 2)
                CurrentSheet.Cells(TargetRow, 1).Resize(.Cells(.Rows.Count, 2).End(xlUp).row - 2, 1) = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(0)
            End With
            .Close False
        End With
        
        
    End If
    
    
Next f
End Sub



Sub 棚卸し用_csv読み込み()

Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject

Dim CSVs As Files
Dim CSV As file
Dim row As Long
Dim data
Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
Dim i As Long
Dim x As Long
Dim out()
Dim BOF As Boolean

reg.Pattern = ",(?=(?:[^""]*""[^""]*"")*[^""]*$)"
reg.Global = True

row = 1
Set CSVs = fso.GetFolder("\\nksv569.ad.nkc.co.jp\Share\mnt\DGS-SV\セットアップレポート\棚卸し本データ\Edit").Files

For Each CSV In CSVs
    Open CSV.Path For Input As #1
    BOF = True
    Do While Not EOF(1)
        Line Input #1, data
        data = Split(reg.Replace(data, "@123@"), "@123@")
        
        If row = 1 And BOF = True Then
        
            'ヘッダの列数記憶
            x = UBound(data)
            'ヘッダ書き出し
            ReDim Preserve out(x, row - 1)
            For i = 0 To UBound(data)
                out(i, row - 1) = Replace(data(i), """", "")
            Next i
            BOF = False
            row = row + 1
            
        ElseIf BOF = True Then
        
            BOF = False
            
        Else
        
            '記憶した列数とあっているか検証
            'If x <> UBound(data) Then Stop
            'データ書き出し
            ReDim Preserve out(x, row - 1)
            For i = 0 To UBound(data)
                out(i, row - 1) = Replace(data(i), """", "")
            Next i
            row = row + 1
            
        End If
        
        DoEvents
    Loop
    
    Close #1
Next CSV

out = WorksheetFunction.Transpose(out)

Range(Cells(1, 1), Cells(UBound(out, 1) - 1, UBound(out, 2) - 1)) = out


End Sub

Sub testttttt()
 
Dim tmp As Variant
Dim AdoCon As ADODB.Connection
Set AdoCon = New ADODB.Connection
AdoCon.CursorLocation = 3 ' クライアントサイドカーソルに変更
AdoCon.Open "Driver={MySQL ODBC 8.0 Unicode Driver};Server=nksv1359.ad.nkc.co.jp;Database=WorkCommunication;User=2prod1-admin;Password=SMTjissoadmin;"
 
Dim rs As Recordset
 
For Each tmp In Selection
    If tmp.Value <> "" Then
        Set rs = AdoCon.Execute("SELECT orderNumber,typeID,Title,Person,TechnicalPerson,PDM_NO FROM ApplyOrder LEFT JOIN register ON ApplyOrder.ItemNumber = register.ItemNumber WHERE orderNumber = '" & tmp.Text & "'")
        Do While rs.EOF = False
            If rs("Title") <> "Null" Then
                tmp.Offset(0, 12).Value = rs("Title")
                tmp.Offset(0, 13).Value = rs("PDM_NO")
            End If
            rs.MoveNext
        Loop
    End If
Next tmp

End Sub

Private Sub SetCalender()
    '■カレンダ セット処理
    Dim wDate       As Date
    Dim wTopNo      As Integer
    Dim wLastDay    As Integer
    Dim wIdx1       As Integer
    Dim wDay        As Integer

    If IsNumeric(Me.txt年) = False Then
        Me.txt年 = year(Date)
    End If
    If Me.txt月 < 1 Or Me.txt月 > 12 Then
        Me.txt月 = month(Date)
    End If
    
    For wIdx1 = 1 To 42
        wArrayDay(wIdx1) = 0
    Next
        
    wDate = DateSerial(Me.txt年, Me.txt月, 1)
    wLastDay = day(DateAdd("d", -1, DateAdd("m", 1, wDate)))
    wTopNo = Format(wDate, "w")
    wIdx1 = wTopNo
    wDay = 1
    For wDay = 1 To wLastDay
        wArrayDay(wIdx1) = wDay
        wIdx1 = wIdx1 + 1
    Next

    For wIdx1 = 1 To 42
        If wArrayDay(wIdx1) <> 0 Then
            Me("日ボタン" & Format(wIdx1, "00")).Caption = Format(wArrayDay(wIdx1), "#")
            Me("日ボタン" & Format(wIdx1, "00")).Enabled = True
        Else
            Me("日ボタン" & Format(wIdx1, "00")).Caption = ""
            Me("日ボタン" & Format(wIdx1, "00")).Enabled = False
        End If
    Next
    
    If year(Date) = Val(Me.txt年) And month(Date) = Val(Me.txt月) Then
        Me("日ボタン" & Format(wTopNo - 1 + day(Date), "00")).SetFocus
    Else
        Me("日ボタン" & Format(wTopNo, "00")).SetFocus
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub ClickDate(prmClnNo)
    '■カレンダの日ボタン選択時の処理
    rtnDate = DateSerial(Me.txt年, Me.txt月, wArrayDay(prmClnNo))
    Unload Me
End Sub

VBA MACRO 差分確認関係.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/差分確認関係'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit


Sub SabunMeisai(Optional r As IRibbonControl)


Dim f As file
Dim FD As Folder
Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject

With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFolderPicker)
    .AllowMultiSelect = False
    If .Show = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not fso.FolderExists(.SelectedItems(1)) Then
        Call MsgBox("指定フォルダが見つかりません。", vbCritical)
        Exit Sub
    Else
        Set FD = fso.GetFolder(.SelectedItems(1))
    End If
End With

Dim buf As String
Dim buf_split() As String
Dim BOSS As Dictionary
Set BOSS = New Dictionary
Dim SMT As Dictionary
Set SMT = New Dictionary
Dim BOSS_SMT As Dictionary
Set BOSS_SMT = New Dictionary
Dim hinmoku As String

Application.ScreenUpdating = False

For Each f In FD.Files
    BOSS.RemoveAll
    SMT.RemoveAll
    BOSS_SMT.RemoveAll
    If fso.GetExtensionName(f.Path) = "csv" Then
        
        Open f.Path For Input As #1
        Line Input #1, buf
        hinmoku = Split(buf, ",")(1)
        Do Until EOF(1)
            Line Input #1, buf
            buf_split = Split(buf, ",")
            If buf_split(1) = Empty Then
                Call SMT.add(buf_split(0), buf_split(2))
            ElseIf buf_split(2) = Empty Then
                Call BOSS.add(buf_split(0), buf_split(1))
            ElseIf buf_split(1) <> buf_split(2) Then
                Call BOSS_SMT.add(buf_split(0), Array(buf_split(1), buf_split(2)))
            End If
            
            DoEvents
        Loop
        Close #1
        
        Dim tmp
        Dim cnt As Long
        Dim max_cnt As Long
        
        DoEvents
        With Workbooks.add
            ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = False
            Sheet_Sabun.Copy .Sheets(1)
            ThisWorkbook.IsAddin = True
            Application.DisplayAlerts = False
            Sheets(2).Delete
            Application.DisplayAlerts = True
            With .Sheets(1)
            
                .Cells(1, 1).Value = hinmoku & "_差分明細"
                .Cells(4, 7).Value = "BOSS"
                .Cells(4, 9).Value = "SMT"
                .Cells(1, 7).Value = fso.GetBaseName(f.Name)
                
                cnt = 4
                For Each tmp In BOSS.keys
                    cnt = cnt + 1
                    .Cells(cnt, 7).Value = Replace(tmp, "'", "")
                    .Cells(cnt, 8).Value = CInt(BOSS(tmp))
                Next tmp
                
                max_cnt = cnt
                
                cnt = 4
                For Each tmp In SMT.keys
                    cnt = cnt + 1
                    .Cells(cnt, 9).Value = Replace(tmp, "'", "")
                    .Cells(cnt, 10).Value = CInt(SMT(tmp))
                Next tmp
                
                If BOSS_SMT.Count > 0 Then
                If max_cnt < cnt Then max_cnt = cnt
                    cnt = max_cnt + 2
                    .Cells(cnt, 7).Value = "★数量差分"
                    
                    For Each tmp In BOSS_SMT.keys
                        cnt = cnt + 1
                        .Cells(cnt, 7).Value = Replace(tmp, "'", "")
                        .Cells(cnt, 8).Value = CInt(BOSS_SMT(tmp)(0))
                        .Cells(cnt, 9).Value = Replace(tmp, "'", "")
                        .Cells(cnt, 10).Value = CInt(BOSS_SMT(tmp)(1))
                    Next tmp
                End If
                
                .Columns(7).AutoFit
                .Columns(9).AutoFit
                Range(Cells(5, 1), Cells(max_cnt, 5)).Borders.LineStyle = True
                
            End With
            
            If fso.FileExists(f.ParentFolder & "\" & hinmoku & "_差分明細.xlsx") Then
                MsgBox "品目名が重複しました｡ファイル名の後ろにランダムな文字列を付与します｡"
                .SaveAs Filename:=f.ParentFolder & "\" & hinmoku & "_差分明細_" & CLng(Format(Now, "mmss")) + Round(Rnd * 1000) & ".xlsx"
            Else
                .SaveAs Filename:=f.ParentFolder & "\" & hinmoku & "_差分明細.xlsx"
            End If
            .Close False
        End With
        
        
        
    End If
    
Next f

Application.ScreenUpdating = True

Shell "explorer.exe """ & FD.Path & """", vbNormalFocus

End Sub



Sub ReadHenkouHinmoku(Optional r As IRibbonControl)

Dim d As Dictionary
Set d = New Dictionary
Dim i As Long

Dim f As file
Dim FD As Folder
Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject

Dim wsh As Object
Set wsh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")

If ActiveSheet.Name <> "差分明細" Then
    MsgBox "対応外のシート"
    End
End If

'Set FD = fso.GetFolder(fso.BuildPath(wsh.SpecialFolders("MyDocuments"), "tmp"))

With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFolderPicker)
    .AllowMultiSelect = False
    If .Show = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not fso.FolderExists(.SelectedItems(1)) Then
        Call MsgBox("指定フォルダが見つかりません。", vbCritical)
        Exit Sub
    Else
        Set FD = fso.GetFolder(.SelectedItems(1))
    End If
End With


Application.ScreenUpdating = False

Dim tmp
Dim flag

For Each f In FD.Files
If InStr(f.Name, "変更品目") <> 0 Then
    
    With Workbooks.Open(f.Path, ReadOnly:=True)
        
        flag = False
        For Each tmp In .Sheets
            If tmp.Name = "指示書" Then flag = True
        Next tmp
        If flag Then
            With .Sheets("指示書")
    
            For i = 8 To .Cells(Rows.Count, 4).End(xlUp).row
                
                If d.Exists(Replace(.Cells(i, 4).Text, "S-", "-")) = False Then
                    d.add Replace(.Cells(i, 4).Text, "S-", "-"), New Dictionary
                End If
                
                Dim hinmoku As String: hinmoku = Replace(.Cells(i, 4).Text, "S-", "-")
                Dim NewItem As String: NewItem = Replace(.Cells(i, 13).Text, "-", "")
                Dim OldItem As String: OldItem = Replace(.Cells(i, 7).Text, "-", "")
                Dim Flg_remove
                Flg_remove = False
                If NewItem = "" Then
                    NewItem = OldItem
                    Flg_remove = True
                End If
                
                If d(hinmoku).Exists(NewItem) Then
                    If Flg_remove Then
                        d(hinmoku)((NewItem)).subcnt
                    Else
                        d(hinmoku)((NewItem)).addcnt
                        If d(hinmoku)((NewItem)).OldItem = "" Then
                            d(hinmoku)((NewItem)).OldItem = OldItem
                        Else
                            If d(hinmoku)((NewItem)).OldItem <> OldItem And OldItem <> "" Then
                                MsgBox "検証エラーが発生しました｡出力結果がおかしい可能性が高いです｡" + vbCrLf + "マクロでは対応できない複雑な変更である可能性があります｡" + vbCrLf + "ECNが特定できるのであれば､ECNをそのまま送付したほうが良いかもしれません｡"
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                Else
                    Dim myChangeItem As ChangeItem
                    Set myChangeItem = New ChangeItem
                    If Flg_remove Then
                        myChangeItem.subcnt
                    Else
                        myChangeItem.addcnt
                        myChangeItem.OldItem = OldItem
                    End If
                    myChangeItem.Code = Replace(StrConv(.Cells(2, 9).Text, vbNarrow), " ", "")
                    d(hinmoku).add NewItem, myChangeItem
                    
                    Set myChangeItem = Nothing
                End If
                    
            Next i
            End With
        Else
            MsgBox f.Name & vbCrLf & "[指示書]シートが見つかりませんでした｡スキップします｡"
        End If
    
    .Close False
    
    End With

End If
Next f

For i = 5 To Cells(Rows.Count, 7).End(xlUp).row

    hinmoku = Mid(Cells(1, 1).Text, 1, InStr(Cells(1, 1).Text, "_差分明細") - 1)
    
    hinmoku = Replace(hinmoku, "S-", "-")
    
    If d.Exists(hinmoku) = False Then
        MsgBox "データ無し"
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If d(hinmoku).Exists(Cells(i, 7).Text) And Cells(i, 8).Value <> "" Then
        
        Cells(i, 1).Value = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).Code
        Cells(i, 2).Value = CStr(Cells(i, 7).Text)
        
        If InStr(CStr(Cells(i, 7).Text), "6190") <> 0 Then
            Cells(i, 5).Value = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt
        Else
            On Error Resume Next
            Dim e As Long
            e = 0
            If d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).OldItem = "" Then
                OldItem = Cells(i, 7).Text
            Else
                OldItem = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).OldItem
            End If
            e = Application.WorksheetFunction.XLookup(OldItem, Columns(9), Columns(10))
            On Error GoTo 0
            If e <> 0 Then
                Dim SmtCnt As Long
                SmtCnt = Application.WorksheetFunction.XLookup(OldItem, Columns(9), Columns(10))
                If d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt <> SmtCnt Then
                    
                    If Cells(i, 7).Text = OldItem Then
                        Cells(i, 3).Value = SmtCnt + d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt_minus
                        Cells(i, 4).Value = OldItem
                        Cells(i, 5).Value = SmtCnt
                    Else
                        '多分間違ってるので処理入ったとき検証 動けばいいやで作ったので頭が回らない。。。
                        Cells(i, 3).Value = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt
                        Cells(i, 4).Value = CStr(d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).OldItem)
                        Cells(i, 5).Value = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt
                        'If Cells(i, 3).Value <> SmtCnt + D(HINMOKU)(Cells(i, 4).Text).cnt_minus Then Stop
                    End If
                Else
                    Cells(i, 3).Value = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt
                    Cells(i, 4).Value = CStr(d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).OldItem)
                    Cells(i, 5).Value = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt
                End If
            Else
                 Cells(i, 3).Value = d(hinmoku)(Cells(i, 7).Text).cnt
                 Cells(i, 4).Value = OldItem
                 Cells(i, 5).Value = "?除外部品か削除部品"
            End If


            If Cells(i, 3).Value <> Cells(i, 8).Value Then
                MsgBox "BOSS差分と変更情報の数量が合いません｡", vbCritical
                Cells(i, 3).Font.Color = 255
                Cells(i, 8).Font.Color = 255
            End If
        End If
    End If
Next i

Application.ScreenUpdating = True


End Sub

Dim KIJUN_DATE As Date
Load カレンダフォーム
カレンダフォーム.Show vbModal
KIJUN_DATE = rtnDate
Unload カレンダフォーム






End Sub

VBA MACRO 実装指示.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/実装指示'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit


Public Sub JissouShiji(Optional r As IRibbonControl)


Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp

Dim cursol1 As Long
Dim cursol2 As Long
Dim i As Long
Dim j As Long
Dim n As Long
Dim tmp
Dim tmp2
Dim str1 As String
Dim str2 As String

Dim ws As Worksheet
Dim moto_ws As Worksheet

'Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic

'ワークシート設定
For Each tmp In Sheets
    If tmp.Name = "job" Then Set moto_ws = tmp
    If tmp.Name = "実装指示" Then Set ws = tmp
Next tmp

Set tmp = Nothing

If ws Is Nothing Or moto_ws Is Nothing Then
    MsgBox "処理対象のシートが見つかりません。"
    Exit Sub
End If

If moto_ws.Cells(moto_ws.Rows.Count, 10).End(xlUp).row < 4 Or moto_ws.Cells(moto_ws.Rows.Count, 16).End(xlUp).row < 4 Then
    MsgBox "データを入力してください。"
    Exit Sub
End If

'シート初期化
Range(ws.Cells(4, 4), ws.Cells(101, 10)) = Null
ws.Columns(4).NumberFormatLocal = "@"

'jobシートからデータコピー(値のみ書式なし)
tmp = Range(moto_ws.Cells(6, 7), moto_ws.Cells(moto_ws.Rows.Count, 10).End(xlUp))
For i = 1 To UBound(tmp, 1)
    tmp(i, 2) = Split(tmp(i, 2), vbLf)(0)
Next i
ws.Cells(4, 4).Resize(UBound(tmp, 1), UBound(tmp, 2)) = tmp

tmp = Range(moto_ws.Cells(6, 14), moto_ws.Cells(moto_ws.Rows.Count, 9).End(xlUp).Offset(0, 7))
ws.Cells(4, 8).Resize(UBound(tmp, 1), UBound(tmp, 2)) = tmp


Set tmp = Nothing


With ws

n = .Cells(Rows.Count, 4).End(xlUp).row - 4

ReDim arr(n) As Item
i = 4


reg.Pattern = "^(.+)～(.+)$"



'品目空欄まで繰り返し
'全データを順番にクラス"Item"型の配列へ格納
Do While .Cells(i, 5).Value <> ""
    str1 = ""
    str2 = ""
    
    If reg.Test(.Cells(i, 8).Text) Then
        str1 = reg.Execute(.Cells(i, 8).Text).Item(0).SubMatches.Item(0)
        str2 = reg.Execute(.Cells(i, 8).Text).Item(0).SubMatches.Item(1)
    Else
        str1 = .Cells(i, 8).Text
    End If
    
    tmp = Range(.Cells(i, 4), .Cells(i, 7))
    
    Set arr(i - 4) = New Item
    arr(i - 4).Cel = tmp
    arr(i - 4).speed = .Cells(i, 1).Text
    arr(i - 4).hinmoku = .Cells(i, 5).Text
    arr(i - 4).print1 = str1
    arr(i - 4).print2 = str2
    arr(i - 4).HDP = .Cells(i, 9).Text
    arr(i - 4).BHDP = .Cells(i, 10).Text


    i = i + 1
    DoEvents
Loop

Set tmp = Nothing


'全部消す
Range(.Cells(4, 4), .Cells(101, 10)) = Null

'配列のカーソル
cursol1 = 0
'出力先のカーソル
cursol2 = 4



For i = 0 To UBound(arr)

    If i <> UBound(arr) Then
        '次の品目の内容をtmpへ格納
        If arr(i + 1).speed = "#N/A" Then
            'NAなら強制的に単品モード
            tmp = ""
        Else
            tmp = arr(i + 1).speed
        End If
        
        tmp2 = arr(i + 1).hinmoku
    Else
        '次の品目なかったら空欄
        tmp = ""
        tmp2 = ""
    End If
    
    If arr(i).speed = tmp And arr(i).KOBETU = False Then
        '個別でも特殊でもなく、スピードが一緒であればカーソルを動かさず保留して次の品目へ
    ElseIf (arr(i).hinmoku = tmp2) Then
        '品目名がある程度一致したら同様。基盤が一緒かどうかで判別すべきだが、実質不可能なので暫定対処。
        '判定方法はクラス内参照
    Else
        
        '2つのカーソルを元にデータを出力
        
        '1つ目の面
        For j = cursol1 To i
            Range(.Cells(cursol2, 4), .Cells(cursol2, 7)) = arr(j).Cel
            .Cells(cursol2, 8).Value = arr(j).print1
            .Cells(cursol2, 9).Value = arr(j).HDP
            cursol2 = cursol2 + 1

        Next j
        
        '2つ目の面
        For j = cursol1 To i
            If arr(j).RYOMEN = True Then
                Range(.Cells(cursol2, 4), .Cells(cursol2, 7)) = arr(j).Cel
                .Cells(cursol2, 8).Value = arr(j).print2
                .Cells(cursol2, 10).Value = arr(j).BHDP
                cursol2 = cursol2 + 1
            End If
        Next j
        
        cursol1 = i + 1
        
        
    End If

DoEvents
Next i

.Range(.Rows(.Cells(.Rows.Count, 5).End(xlUp).row + 1), .Rows(.Rows.Count)).Delete


End With


Application.ScreenUpdating = True


End Sub

VBA MACRO Ribbon.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/Ribbon'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit

'WinAPI宣言 64Bitの場合PtrSafe
#If VBA7 And Win64 Then
  Private Declare PtrSafe Sub MoveMemory Lib "kernel32" Alias "RtlMoveMemory" (pDest As Any, pSrc As Any, ByVal cbLen As LongPtr)
#Else
'32Bit
  Private Declare PtrSafe Sub MoveMemory Lib "kernel32" Alias "RtlMoveMemory" (pDest As Any, pSrc As Any, ByVal cbLen As Long)
#End If

'リボンオブジェクト
Private myRibbon As IRibbonUI

Public DebugFlag As Boolean

'リボン読み込み時実行
Public Sub Ribbon_onLoad(Ribbon As IRibbonUI)
'グローバル変数へ格納
Set myRibbon = Ribbon
'何かの拍子に変数消えた時のためにメモリアドレスをシートへ格納
Settings.Range("RibbonAddress").Value = CStr(ObjPtr(Ribbon))
End Sub

'リボンロード時・再描画時実行 隠しコマンド表示フラグ
Public Sub debug_getVisible(control As IRibbonControl, ByRef returnedVal)
returnedVal = DebugFlag
End Sub


Public Sub DebugMenuVisible(f As Boolean)
'変数消えてたら
If myRibbon Is Nothing Then
    'メモリアドレスからオブジェクトを取得
    Set myRibbon = GetRibbon(yTOOL_Option.Range("RibbonAddress").Value)
End If

DebugFlag = f
'再描画　ステップインで実行するとExcel死ぬので注意
myRibbon.Invalidate
End Sub

'アドレスからオブジェクトを取得
'64Bit長
#If VBA7 And Win64 Then
Private Function GetRibbon(ByVal lRibbonPointer As LongPtr) As Object
    Dim P As LongPtr
#Else
'32Bit長
Private Function GetRibbon(ByVal lRibbonPointer As Long) As Object
      Dim P As Long
#End If

Dim ribbonObj As Object
'移動元,移動先メモリアドレス,メモリサイズ
MoveMemory ribbonObj, lRibbonPointer, LenB(lRibbonPointer)
'戻り値
Set GetRibbon = ribbonObj
'後始末
P = 0: MoveMemory ribbonObj, P, LenB(P)
End Function

in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/crb読む'

Option Explicit

Sub asda()

Sub Read_crb(a As String)
'Dim STRM As Object
'Set STRM = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
Dim STRM As ADODB.Stream
Set STRM = New ADODB.Stream
Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject
Dim buf As String, target As String

Dim TMP_Folder As String
Dim TMP_crb As String

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
Dim reg2 As RegExp
Set reg2 = New RegExp
reg.Pattern = "^\[LotNames]$"
reg2.Pattern = "^\[PositionData<(\d+)>\]$"


'Target = "C:\Users\ys97534\Documents\tmp\220420-001.crb"
target = a


TMP_Folder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(TemporaryFolder)
TMP_crb = fso.BuildPath(TMP_Folder, "crb.txt")

Dim JobName As String
JobName = fso.GetBaseName(target)

'ADODB.Streamさんは未知の拡張子だとエラー返すようなのでユーザーディレクトリのtmpフォルダへコピーしてリネーム
fso.CopyFile target, TMP_crb


Dim PCB_Name As String
ReDim orderNumbers(0) As orderNumber
Dim num As Long
Dim Symbol_Name As String
Dim d As Dictionary
Set d = New Dictionary
Dim Lot As String

Dim D_Err As Dictionary
Set D_Err = New Dictionary

With STRM
.Charset = "UTF-8"
.Open
.LoadFromFile TMP_crb

'最終行まで
Do Until .EOS
    
    '1行進める
    buf = .ReadText(-2)
    
    '[LotNames]
    If reg.Test(buf) Then
        .ReadText (-2)
        reg.Pattern = "^""000(\d{9})_(.+)_(P\d+)""$"
        buf = .ReadText(-2)
        'LOTNUM順にorderNumberオブジェクト生成､指図を入れる
        Do Until buf = ""
            Lot = Split(buf, " ")(2)
            num = CLng(Split(buf, " ")(1) - 1)
            If reg.Test(Lot) Then
                If UBound(orderNumbers) < num Then
                    ReDim Preserve orderNumbers(num)
                End If
                Set orderNumbers(num) = New orderNumber
                orderNumbers(num).Name = reg.Execute(Lot)(0).SubMatches(0)
                orderNumbers(num).JobName = JobName
                orderNumbers(num).TypeID = reg.Execute(Lot)(0).SubMatches(1)
                orderNumbers(num).Pn = reg.Execute(Lot)(0).SubMatches(2)
                orderNumbers(num).ver = Replace(Split(buf, " ")(11), """", "")
            Else
                'MsgBox Lot + vbCrLf + "Err"
                If Not D_Err.Exists(num) Then D_Err.add num, True
                'Stop
            End If
            buf = .ReadText(-2)
        Loop
    End If
    
    '[PositionData<[LOTNUM]>]
    If reg2.Test(buf) Then
        '対応するLOTNUMを格納
        num = CLng(reg2.Execute(buf)(0).SubMatches(0)) - 1
        .ReadText (-2)
        buf = .ReadText(-2)
        '空白行まで読み続ける
        Do Until buf = ""
            '対応するLOTNUMのorderNumber.Dへ格納 key=シンボル　item=PARTSID
            Symbol_Name = CStr(Replace(Split(buf, " ")(24), """", ""))
            If Symbol_Name <> "SIKAKU" And Symbol_Name <> "" And D_Err(num) = "" Then
                If orderNumbers(num).d.Exists(Symbol_Name) Then
                    If orderNumbers(num).d(Symbol_Name) <> Split(buf, " ")(5) Then
                        MsgBox "Err"
                        Stop
                    End If
                Else
                    orderNumbers(num).d.add Symbol_Name, Split(buf, " ")(5)
                End If
            End If
            buf = .ReadText(-2)
        Loop
    End If
    
    'PartsDataの辞書作成 key=PARTSID key=部品コード
    If buf = "[PartsData]" Then
        .ReadText (-2)
        buf = .ReadText(-2)
        Do Until buf = ""
            d.add Split(buf, " ")(0), CStr(Replace(Split(buf, " ")(1), """", ""))
            buf = .ReadText(-2)
        Loop
        
        
    End If
    DoEvents
Loop
.Close
End With





Dim tmp
Dim i As Long
Dim strSQL As String

Dim AdoCon As ADODB.Connection
Set AdoCon = New ADODB.Connection
AdoCon.CursorLocation = adUseServer ' クライアントサイドカーソルに変更
AdoCon.Open "Driver={MySQL ODBC 8.0 Unicode Driver};Server=nksv569.ad.nkc.co.jp;Database=QCD2;User=yTOOLS;Password=yTOOLS;"

Dim rs As Recordset
Set rs = New Recordset

Dim First_Flag As Boolean
Dim Skip_Flag As Boolean
Dim SecondOrderNumber_flag As Boolean

Dim D2 As Dictionary
Set D2 = New Dictionary


For i = 0 To UBound(orderNumbers) - 1
    DoEvents
    Debug.Print (CStr(i) + "/" + CStr(UBound(orderNumbers) - 1))
    
    If Not D_Err(i) Then
    
        If Not D2.Exists(orderNumbers(i).Name) Then
            D2.add orderNumbers(i).Name, orderNumbers(i).Name
            SecondOrderNumber_flag = False
        Else
            SecondOrderNumber_flag = True
        End If
        
        Skip_Flag = False
        
        Set rs = AdoCon.Execute("SELECT DISTINCT orderNumber FROM orderNumberBOM WHERE orderNumber=""" + orderNumbers(i).Name + """;")
        If rs.EOF = False And SecondOrderNumber_flag = False Then
            'If MsgBox("指図が重複しました｡[" + orderNumbers(i).Name + "]" + vbCrLf + "既存の指図のレコードを全て削除して上書きしますか?", vbYesNo) = vbYes Then
                Call AdoCon.Execute("DELETE FROM orderNumberBOM WHERE orderNumber ='" & orderNumbers(i).Name & "'")
                Call AdoCon.Execute("UPDATE orderNumberBOM_Log SET Deleted = 1 WHERE orderNumber ='" & orderNumbers(i).Name & "';")
            'Else
            '    If MsgBox("飛ばして処理を続行しますか?", vbOKCancel) = vbOK Then
            '        Skip_Flag = True
            '    Else
            '        MsgBox "処理を中止します｡"
            '        Exit Sub
            '    End If
            'End If
        End If
        
        
        If Skip_Flag = False Then
            First_Flag = True
            For Each tmp In orderNumbers(i).d
                
                If First_Flag Then
                    strSQL = "INSERT INTO orderNumberBOM(orderNumber, Symbol, PartNo)  VALUES"
                    strSQL = strSQL + "(""" + orderNumbers(i).Name + """, """ + tmp + """, """ + d(orderNumbers(i).d(tmp)) + """)"
                    First_Flag = False
                Else
                    strSQL = strSQL + ",(""" + orderNumbers(i).Name + """, """ + tmp + """, """ + d(orderNumbers(i).d(tmp)) + """ )"
                End If
                DoEvents
            Next tmp
            Call AdoCon.Execute(strSQL)
            
            strSQL = "INSERT INTO orderNumberBOM_Log(orderNumber, JobName, TypeID, Pn, Ver) VALUES("
            strSQL = strSQL + "'" + orderNumbers(i).Name + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).JobName + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).TypeID + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).Pn + "', '" + orderNumbers(i).ver + "');"
            
            Call AdoCon.Execute(strSQL)
            
        End If
    End If
Next i





AdoCon.Close
Set AdoCon = Nothing


End Sub

VBA MACRO 品目選定ADO.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/品目選定ADO'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit

Sub Kyotsukakunin_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)

Dim i As Long
Dim rng As Range

Dim start_row As Long
Dim end_row As Long
Dim col As Long
Dim cnt As Long

start_row = Selection(1).row
end_row = Selection(Selection.Count).row
col = Selection(1).Column

Dim tmp
tmp = Range(Cells(start_row, 1), Cells(end_row, 1))
Dim tmp2
tmp2 = Range(Cells(start_row, 2), Cells(end_row, 2))

If MsgBox("処理に時間がかかります!", vbInformation + vbOKCancel) = vbCancel Then End


cnt = 1

For i = start_row To end_row - 2
    
    If i = start_row Then
        Range(Cells(end_row, col), Cells(i + 1, col)).Select
        Debug.Print Cells(i, Selection(1).Column)
        Call BuhinKakunin_ADO(start_row, end_row)
        tmp(cnt, 1) = Cells(i, 1).Value
        tmp2(cnt, 1) = Cells(i, 2).Value
    End If
    
    
    Union(Range(Cells(start_row, col), Cells(i, col)), Range(Cells(end_row, col), Cells(i + 2, col))).Select
    Debug.Print Cells(i, Selection(1).Column)
    Call BuhinKakunin_ADO(start_row, end_row)
    tmp(cnt + 1, 1) = Cells(i + 1, 1).Value
    tmp2(cnt + 1, 1) = Cells(i + 1, 2).Value
    
    If i = end_row - 2 Then
        Range(Cells(start_row, col), Cells(i + 1, col)).Select
        Call BuhinKakunin_ADO(start_row, end_row)
        tmp(cnt + 2, 1) = Cells(i + 2, 1).Value
        tmp2(cnt + 2, 1) = Cells(i + 2, 2).Value
    End If
    
    
    cnt = cnt + 1
    
Next i

Columns(1).ClearContents
Columns(2).ClearContents
Range(Cells(start_row, 1), Cells(end_row, 1)) = tmp
Range(Cells(start_row, 2), Cells(end_row, 2)) = tmp2

End Sub
