Sub BuhinKakunin_ADO(Optional s As Long = 1, Optional e As Long = 300, Optional msgFlag As Boolean = False)

Dim hinmoku As Dictionary
Set hinmoku = New Dictionary
Dim tmp As Variant
Dim i As Long
Dim cnt As Double


Dim DB_Path As String
DB_Path = "\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\Job作成\部品リスト.accdb"

Dim con As ADODB.Connection
Dim rs As ADODB.Recordset
Dim rs2 As ADODB.Recordset

Set con = New ADODB.Connection

con.mode = adModeRead
con.ConnectionString = "Provider=Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.16.0;" _
                        & "Data Source=" & DB_Path & ""
con.Open


Dim strSQL As String

'品目リスト取得
For Each tmp In Selection
    '存在チェック
    Set rs = con.Execute("SELECT DISTINCT 品目名 FROM egproBOM WHERE 品目名='" & tmp.Text & "';")
    If rs.EOF And tmp.Text <> "" Then
        If MsgBox("部品データが存在しない品目が選択されています!" & vbCrLf & tmp.Text & vbCrLf & "処理を続行しますか?", vbExclamation + vbYesNo) = vbNo Then
            End
        End If
    End If
    If tmp.Text <> "" Then
        If strSQL = "" Then
            strSQL = "'" & tmp.Text & "'"
        Else
            strSQL = strSQL & ",'" & tmp.Text & "'"
        End If
        'rs.Close インスタンス破棄(Set rs =)で無理やり高速化…
    End If
Next tmp

rs.Close
Set rs = Nothing


'JOBの部品リストを辞書型で作成。ITEMに詳細情報をClassで作成。
Dim JOB_HINMOKU As Dictionary
Set JOB_HINMOKU = New Dictionary
Dim myBUHIN As BUHIN_Class
Dim HINMOKU_SIZE As Double

Set rs = con.Execute("SELECT DISTINCT egproBOM.部品名, PartList.供給種別, PartList.ノズル, PartList.フィーダーサイズ " & _
                     "FROM egproBOM LEFT JOIN PartList ON (egproBOM.部品名 = PartList.部品名)" & _
                     "WHERE egproBOM.品目名 IN (" & strSQL & ");")

Dim DBG_3H As Double
Dim DBG_other3H As Double

Do Until rs.EOF

    Set myBUHIN = New BUHIN_Class
    myBUHIN.部品名 = CStr(rs.Fields("部品名"))
    If IsNull(rs.Fields("供給種別")) Then
        myBUHIN.供給種別 = ""
        myBUHIN.ノズル = ""
        myBUHIN.フィーダーサイズ = 0
    Else
        myBUHIN.供給種別 = CStr(rs.Fields("供給種別"))
        myBUHIN.ノズル = CStr(rs.Fields("ノズル"))
        myBUHIN.フィーダーサイズ = CDbl(rs.Fields("フィーダーサイズ"))
    End If
    JOB_HINMOKU.add CStr(rs.Fields("部品名")), myBUHIN
    
    HINMOKU_SIZE = HINMOKU_SIZE + CDbl(myBUHIN.フィーダーサイズ)
        
    Dim str
    For Each str In Split(CStr(myBUHIN.ノズル), ",")
        If CLng(str) > 1000 Then
            DBG_3H = DBG_3H + CDbl(myBUHIN.フィーダーサイズ)
            Exit For
        End If
        'If CLng(str) < 1000 Then
        '    DBG_other3H = DBG_other3H + CDbl(rs.Fields("フィーダーサイズ"))
        '    Exit For
        'End If
    Next str
    
    rs.MoveNext
Loop

rs.Close
Set rs = Nothing
Set rs2 = Nothing



With ActiveWorkbook.Sheets("Job作成")
'With Workbooks("Book8").Sheets("未整備なし")

Dim col As Long
col = .Rows(1).Find("品目", LookIn:=xlValues, LookAt:=xlWhole).Column
If col < 6 Then
    MsgBox "マクロ動作列がありません｡"
    End
End If


'Executeだと読み取り専用/前方スクロールカーソル固定なので機能が制限される
Set rs = New ADODB.Recordset
rs.CursorLocation = adUseClient
rs.CursorType = adOpenStatic
rs.LockType = adLockReadOnly

Dim cnt_3H As Double
Dim cnt_Other3H As Double

ReDim TMP_out(1 To 300, 1 To 2)
For i = s To e '.Cells(.Rows.Count, col).End(xlUp).Row
    cnt = 0
    
    If .Cells(i, col).Text <> "" Then
        'サブクエリでフィルターするべき　いつか修正しよう...
        rs.Open "SELECT egproBOM.部品名, PartList.フィーダーサイズ FROM egproBOM INNER JOIN PartList ON egproBOM.部品名=PartList.部品名 WHERE 品目名='" & .Cells(i, col).Text & "';", con
        If rs.EOF Then
            TMP_out(i, 1) = "?"
        Else
            Do Until rs.EOF
                If JOB_HINMOKU.Exists(CStr(rs.Fields("部品名"))) Then
                    cnt = cnt + CDbl(rs.Fields("フィーダーサイズ"))
                    'DoEvents
                Else
                    
                End If
                rs.MoveNext
            Loop
            
            
            Set rs2 = con.Execute("SELECT SUM(PartList.フィーダーサイズ) AS N FROM egproBOM INNER JOIN PartList ON egproBOM.部品名=PartList.部品名 WHERE 品目名='" & .Cells(i, col).Text & "';")
            
            If CDbl(rs2.Fields("N")) <> 0 Then
                TMP_out(i, 1) = Round(cnt / CDbl(rs2.Fields("N")) * 100) & "%(" & cnt & ")"
            Else
                TMP_out(i, 1) = "0%(トレイ?)"
            End If
            TMP_out(i, 2) = CDbl(rs2.Fields("N")) - cnt
            rs2.Close
        End If
        rs.Close
        'DoEvents
    End If

Next i
Range(.Cells(1, 1), .Cells(UBound(TMP_out, 1), UBound(TMP_out, 2))) = TMP_out

Sub PrintJob_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)

Dim Step2 As Boolean

    MsgBox "マクロ動作列がありません｡"
    End
End If

Step2 = PrintSelect.CB_Step2

j = j - 1

    ws.Columns(12).Hidden = True
    ws.Columns(13).Hidden = True

    ws.Columns(13).Hidden = False

    shp.Delete

Range(ws.Columns(1), ws.Columns(4)).Delete

            Link:=False, DisplayAsIcon:=False, _

            Height:=34)
            objB.Object.Style = 6

            objB.Object.Value = ws.Cells(i, 5).Text
            ShiftColumn = ShiftColumn + 1
            If ShiftColumn = 6 Then
                ShiftColumn = 0
                ShiftRow = ShiftRow + 1
            End If

    ws.Rows(i).AutoFit

        ws.Rows(i).RowHeight = 35
    End If
    
Next i

ws.Parent.Close False

Next tmp

Exit Sub

e:
If err.Number = 1004 Then
    If MsgBox("貼り付け失敗｡" & vbCrLf & "ActiveXが有効になっていることを確認してください。" & vbCrLf & "また、会社の監視アプリの影響で稀に失敗することもあります" & vbCrLf & "リトライしますか?", vbYesNo) = vbYes Then
        Resume
    End If
Else
    MsgBox "謎のエラー"
End If

If Not ws Is Nothing Then ws.Parent.Close False

Exit Sub

End Sub


Sub KIBAN_Mail_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)

Dim tmp As Range

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp

Dim i As Long

Dim Ws_out As Worksheet
Dim cWs As Worksheet
Set cWs = ActiveSheet

Dim Prev As String
Prev = ""

Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject


'Dim TMP_Path As String
'TMP_Path = fso.GetSpecialFolder(TemporaryFolder).Path & "\基板出庫.xlsm"


'If fso.FileExists(TMP_Path) Then
'    Call fso.DeleteFile(TMP_Path)
'End If

If MsgBox("Job名部分を選択していますか?", vbYesNo) = vbNo Then
    MsgBox "中断"
    Exit Sub
End If

reg.Pattern = "^\d{6}-\w{2}(\d{1})$"
Set Ws_out = Workbooks.Open("\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\Job作成\マクロ等\bin\基板出庫.xlsm", ReadOnly:=True).Sheets("基板出庫")

Ws_out.Parent.RefreshAll

cWs.Activate

Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Application.Calculation = xlManual

Dim FN As String


For Each tmp In Selection
    If FN = "" Then
        FN = Left(tmp.Value, 6)
    Else
        If FN <> Left(tmp.Value, 6) Then
            MsgBox "Job名に複数日付が存在します｡処理を終了します｡"
            Application.Calculation = xlAutomatic
            Ws_out.Parent.Close False
            Application.ScreenUpdating = True
            Exit Sub
        End If
    End If
    i = 0
    If reg.Test(tmp.Text) Then
        Do While tmp.Offset(i + 1, 3).Value <> ""
            i = i + 1
        Loop
        If Prev <> reg.Execute(tmp.Text)(0).SubMatches(0) Then
            Ws_out.Cells(Ws_out.Rows.Count, 2).End(xlUp).Offset(1, 0) = "L" & reg.Execute(tmp.Text)(0).SubMatches(0)
            Prev = reg.Execute(tmp.Text)(0).SubMatches(0)
        End If
        Range(tmp.Offset(1, 2), tmp.Offset(i, 6)).Copy Ws_out.Cells(Ws_out.Rows.Count, 2).End(xlUp).Offset(1, 0)

    End If


Next tmp

Application.Run Ws_out.Parent.Name & "!" & "Sheet1.Main"
Application.Calculation = xlAutomatic

If fso.FileExists("\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\AccessDB\SMT_Schedule2\基盤出庫リスト\基盤出庫_" & FN & ".xlsm") Then
    If MsgBox("保存先に既にファイルがあります｡上書きしますか?", vbYesNo) = vbYes Then
        fso.DeleteFile "\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\AccessDB\SMT_Schedule2\基盤出庫リスト\基盤出庫_" & FN & ".xlsm"
    Else
        MsgBox "中断"
        Ws_out.Parent.Close False
        Application.ScreenUpdating = True
        Exit Sub
    End If
End If



Ws_out.SaveAs "\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\AccessDB\SMT_Schedule2\基盤出庫リスト\基盤出庫_" & FN & ".xlsm"
Ws_out.Parent.Close False
Application.ScreenUpdating = True

MsgBox "完了" & vbCrLf & "ファイル内容が問題ないか一応確認してください!"
Shell "C:\Windows\Explorer.exe " & "\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\AccessDB\SMT_Schedule2\基盤出庫リスト", vbNormalFocus


End Sub

VBA MACRO メルセンヌ・ツイスタ.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/メルセンヌ・ツイスタ'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
' ＶＢＡによるメルセンヌツイスタ

Option Explicit


' システムを起動してからの時間をミリ秒単位で返す
' http://msdn.microsoft.com/ja-jp/library/cc429827.aspx
Private Declare PtrSafe Function GetTickCount Lib "kernel32" () As Long


' メルセンヌツイスタのパラメータ(ダイナミッククリエーターの結果)
Private Const MTN = 644, MTM = 322, MTA = 12, MTB = 7, MTC = 15, MTD = 18
Private Const MXA = &H70C20000, UMK = &H78000000, LMK = &H7FFFFFF
Private Const MKB = &H73736B80, MKC = &H6ED28000


' 補助的な定数の宣言
Private Const MTL = MTN - MTM, MTK = MTN - 1, MTJ = MTL - 1, MTP = MTN - 2
Private Const PWA = 2 ^ MTA, PWB = 2 ^ MTB, PWC = 2 ^ MTC, PWD = 2 ^ MTD
Private Const KB = MKB \ PWB, KC = MKC \ PWC
Private Const P32 = 2# ^ 32, P31 = 2 ^ 31, P22 = 2# ^ 22, P9 = 2 ^ 9
Private Const M53 = 2# ^ -53, M32 = 2# ^ -32, M30 = 2# ^ -30


' 乱数の状態
Private mt(0 To MTK), mti As Long


' 初期化の補助関数
Private Function Ri(ByRef r As Double, ByVal i As Long) As Long
    Dim s As Variant
    Dim shft As Double
    Dim a As Long
    If r >= P31 Then a = r - P32 Else a = r
    a = a Xor Int(r * M30)
    If a < 0 Then r = a + P32 Else r = a
    s = 1812433253 * CDec(r) + i: r = s - CDec(Int(s * M32)) * P32
    If r >= P31 Then Ri = r - P31 Else Ri = r
End Function


' s を種にして乱数を初期化する
Public Sub InitMt(ByVal s As Long)
    Dim r As Double
    mt(0) = s And &H7FFFFFFF
    If s < 0 Then r = P32 + s Else r = s
    For mti = 1 To MTK: mt(mti) = Ri(r, mti): Next mti
    mti = MTN
End Sub


' 31 ビットの整数乱数
Public Function NextMt() As Long
    Dim y, k As Long
    If mti = 0 Then InitMt (1)
    If mti = MTN Then
        mti = 0
        For k = 0 To MTJ
            y = (mt(k) And UMK) Or (mt(k + 1) And LMK)
            mt(k) = mt(k + MTM) Xor (y \ 2) Xor (-(y And 1) And MXA)
        Next k
        For k = MTL To MTP
            y = (mt(k) And UMK) Or (mt(k + 1) And LMK)
            mt(k) = mt(k - MTL) Xor (y \ 2) Xor (-(y And 1) And MXA)
        Next k
        y = (mt(MTK) And UMK) Or (mt(0) And LMK)
        mt(MTK) = mt(MTM - 1) Xor (y \ 2) Xor (-(y And 1) And MXA)
    End If
    y = mt(mti): mti = mti + 1
    y = y Xor (y \ PWA): y = y Xor ((y And KB) * PWB)
    y = y Xor ((y And KC) * PWC): y = y Xor (y \ PWD): NextMt = y
End Function


' 0 以上 1 未満の乱数を返す
Public Function NextUnifMt() As Double
    Dim x As Long
    x = NextMt \ P9: NextUnifMt = (NextMt * P22 + x) * M53
End Function


' 時間を種にして乱数を初期化する
Public Sub RandomizeMt()
    InitMt (GetTickCount())
End Sub






-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
VBA MACRO 未整備確認.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/未整備確認'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit

Public rtnDate As Variant


Sub MiseibiKakunin_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)


Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject
Dim file As file
Dim tmp As Variant


If ActiveSheet.Name <> "確認前" Then
    MsgBox "対応外のシート"
    Exit Sub
End If


With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFilePicker)
    .AllowMultiSelect = False
    If .Show = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Set file = fso.GetFile(.SelectedItems(1))
End With

For Each tmp In Workbooks
    If tmp.Name = file.Name Then
        MsgBox "既に開かれています｡閉じてから実行願います｡"
        Exit Sub
    End If
Next tmp


Dim KIJUN_DATE As Date
'Load カレンダフォーム
'カレンダフォーム.Show vbModal
'KIJUN_DATE = rtnDate
'Unload カレンダフォーム



Dim OpenFileData As Variant

With Workbooks.Open(file.Name, ReadOnly:=True)
    OpenFileData = .Sheets(1).UsedRange
    .Close False
End With

KIJUN_DATE = Replace(OpenFileData(3, 4), ".", "/")


Dim COL_SASIZU As Long: COL_SASIZU = 0
Dim COL_HINMOKU As Long: COL_HINMOKU = 0
Dim COL_HINMOKU_TEXT As Long: COL_HINMOKU = 0
Dim i As Long
Dim j As Long

For i = 1 To UBound(OpenFileData, 2)
    Select Case OpenFileData(10, i)
    Case "指図"
        COL_SASIZU = i
    Case "品目"
        COL_HINMOKU = i
    End Select
    
    If InStr(OpenFileData(13, i), "ﾀﾞﾐ-ﾖｳ") <> 0 Then
        COL_HINMOKU_TEXT = i
    End If
    
Next i

COL_HINMOKU_TEXT = 6

If COL_HINMOKU = 0 Or COL_SASIZU = 0 Or COL_HINMOKU_TEXT = 0 Then Stop

Dim d As Dictionary
Set d = New Dictionary

For i = 13 To UBound(OpenFileData, 1)
    
    If OpenFileData(i, COL_HINMOKU) <> "" And InStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_HINMOKU_TEXT), "ﾀﾞﾐ-ﾖｳ") = 0 Then
        If d.Exists(CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_SASIZU))) Then
            If Not d(CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_SASIZU))).Exists(CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_HINMOKU))) Then
                d(CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_SASIZU))).add CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_HINMOKU)), CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_HINMOKU))
            End If
        Else
            Dim myD As Dictionary
            Set myD = New Dictionary
            d.add CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_SASIZU)), myD
            d(CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_SASIZU))).add CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_HINMOKU)), CStr(OpenFileData(i, COL_HINMOKU))
            Set myD = Nothing
        End If
    End If
Next i

Application.CutCopyMode = False

With Workbooks("段取り予定.xlsx").Sheets("確認前")
    .Columns(1).Insert
    For i = 2 To .Cells(.Rows.Count, 5).End(xlUp).row
        If .Cells(i, 6).Text <> "" Then
            If d.Exists(CStr(.Cells(i, 6).Value)) Then
                For Each tmp In d(CStr(.Cells(i, 6).Value))
                    If .Cells(i, 1).Value = "" Then
                        .Cells(i, 1).Value = CStr(tmp)
                    Else
                        .Cells(i, 1).Value = .Cells(i, 1).Value & vbCrLf & CStr(tmp)
                    End If
                Next tmp
                'If .Cells(i, 8).Value > Application.WorksheetFunction.WorkDay(Date, 2) Then
                If .Cells(i, 8).Value > KIJUN_DATE Then
                    Cells(i, 1).Value = Cells(i, 1).Value & vbCrLf & "着手日まだ"
                End If
            Else
                'If .Cells(i, 8).Value > Application.WorksheetFunction.WorkDay(Date, 2) Then
                If .Cells(i, 8).Value > KIJUN_DATE Then
                    Cells(i, 1).Value = "着手日まだ"
                Else
                    Cells(i, 1).Value = "○"
                End If
            End If
        End If
        
    Next i
    .Columns(1).ColumnWidth = 30
    .Columns(1).AutoFit
    .Rows.AutoFit
    
End With
    





End Sub

VBA MACRO ECN.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/ECN'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit

Sub ECN()

Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject

Dim ECN_Link As String
ECN_Link = "\\nksv250.ad.nkc.co.jp\富岡（株）_実装\ECN"

Dim f As file
Dim cnt As Long: cnt = 1

Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
reg.Pattern = "(^ECN.+)\(0864-(\d{6})([ |.]\d{6})?([ |.]\d{6})?\)\((.+)\)\.xlsx?$"


For Each f In fso.GetFolder(ECN_Link).Files
    
    If reg.Test(f.Name) Then
         Cells(cnt, 1).Value = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(0)
         Cells(cnt, 2).Value = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(4)
         Cells(cnt, 3).Value = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(1)
         Cells(cnt, 4).Value = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(2)
         Cells(cnt, 5).Value = reg.Execute(f.Name)(0).SubMatches(3)
    Else
        Cells(cnt, 1).Value = f.Name
    End If
    cnt = cnt + 1
    
Next f

End Sub

VBA MACRO Module5.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/Module5'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit


Sub csv読み込み()

Dim fso As FileSystemObject
Set fso = New FileSystemObject

Dim CSVs As Files
Dim CSV As file
Dim row As Long
Dim data
Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp
Dim i As Long
Dim x As Long
Dim out()
Dim BOF As Boolean

reg.Pattern = ",(?=(?:[^""]*""[^""]*"")*[^""]*$)"
reg.Global = True

row = 1
Set CSVs = fso.GetFolder("E:\RC").Files

For Each CSV In CSVs
    Open CSV.Path For Input As #1
    BOF = True
    Do While Not EOF(1)
        Line Input #1, data
        data = Split(reg.Replace(data, "@123@"), "@123@")
        
        If row = 1 And BOF = True Then
        
            'ヘッダの列数記憶
            x = UBound(data)
            'ヘッダ書き出し
            ReDim Preserve out(x, row - 1)
            For i = 0 To UBound(data)
                out(i, row - 1) = Replace(data(i), """", "")
            Next i
            BOF = False
            row = row + 1
            
        ElseIf BOF = True Then
        
            BOF = False
            
        Else
        
            '記憶した列数とあっているか検証
            'If x <> UBound(data) Then Stop
            'データ書き出し
            ReDim Preserve out(x, row - 1)
            For i = 0 To UBound(data)
                out(i, row - 1) = Replace(data(i), """", "")
            Next i
            row = row + 1
            
        End If
        
        DoEvents
    Loop
    
    Close #1
Next CSV

out = WorksheetFunction.Transpose(out)

Range(Cells(1, 1), Cells(UBound(out, 1) - 1, UBound(out, 2) - 1)) = out


End Sub

Sub Sort_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)

Dim i As Long
Dim col

With ActiveSheet

col = .Rows(1).Find("品目", LookIn:=xlValues, LookAt:=xlWhole).Column

If col < 5 Then
    MsgBox "数式列がありません｡"
    Exit Sub
End If

For i = Selection(1).row To Selection(Selection.Count).row
    If InStr(.Cells(i, col + 5).Text, "鉛フリー") <> 0 Then
        If InStr(.Cells(i, col - 2).Text, "特殊") <> 0 Or InStr(.Cells(i, col - 10).Text, "特殊") Then
            .Cells(i, 1).Value = 5
        ElseIf InStr(.Cells(i, col - 10).Text, "個別") <> 0 Then
            .Cells(i, 1).Value = CLng(.Cells(i, col - 1).Value)
        ElseIf .Cells(i, col - 1).Value <> "試作" Then
            If InStr(.Cells(i, col + 6), "～") <> 0 Then
                .Cells(i, 1).Value = CLng(.Cells(i, col - 1).Value) + 2
            Else
                .Cells(i, 1).Value = CLng(.Cells(i, col - 1).Value) + 1
            End If
        End If
    Else
        If InStr(.Cells(i, col - 2).Value, "③") <> 0 Then
            .Cells(i, 1).Value = 4 + CLng(.Cells(i, col - 1).Value) * 0.00001
        ElseIf InStr(.Cells(i, col - 2).Value, "②") <> 0 Then
            .Cells(i, 1).Value = 2 + CLng(.Cells(i, col - 1).Value) * 0.00001
        ElseIf InStr(.Cells(i, col - 2).Value, "特殊") <> 0 Then
            .Cells(i, 1).Value = 1 + CLng(.Cells(i, col - 1).Value) * 0.00001
        ElseIf .Cells(i, col - 1).Value <> "試作" Then
            .Cells(i, 1).Value = 3 + CLng(.Cells(i, col - 1).Value) * 0.00001
        End If
    End If
    
    If .Cells(i, col - 1).Value = "" Then
        .Cells(i, 1).Value = 997
    End If
    If Left(.Cells(i, col + 1).Value, 2) = "11" Then
        .Cells(i, 1).Value = 998
        With .Cells(i, col - 2).Font
            .Bold = True
            .Size = 14
            .Color = 16777215
            .Parent.Interior.Color = 16711680
            If .Parent.Value = "" Then
                .Parent.Value = "初ロット" & vbCrLf & "温度プロファイル取得"
            Else
                .Parent.Value = "初ロット" & vbCrLf & "温度プロファイル取得" & vbCrLf & .Parent.Value
            End If
            .Parent.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        End With
    End If
    If Left(.Cells(i, col + 1).Value, 2) = "12" Or Left(.Cells(i, col + 1).Value, 2) = "13" Then
        .Cells(i, 1).Value = 999
        With .Cells(i, col - 1).Font
            .Bold = True
            .Size = 12
            .Color = 16777215
            .Parent.Interior.Color = 0
            .Parent.Value = "試作"
            .Parent.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        End With
        .Cells(i, col - 2).Value = "(担当手配*種)"
        With .Cells(i, col - 3).Font
            .Bold = True
            .Size = 10
            .Color = 16711680
            .Parent.Value = "試作"
            .Parent.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        End With
        With .Cells(i, col).Font
            .Bold = True
            .Size = 10
            .Color = 66047
        End With
        With .Cells(i, col + 3)
            .Font.Bold = True
            .NumberFormatLocal = "aaa""曜日"""
        End With
    End If
    Select Case .Cells(i, col).Value
        Case "UR-0511", "UR-0511-01", "URS-0511-01"
            .Cells(i, 1).Value = 500
    End Select
Next i


.Sort.SortFields.Clear
.Sort.SortFields.Add2 key:=Range(.Cells(Selection(1).row, 1), .Cells(Selection(Selection.Count).row, 1)) _
    , SortOn:=xlSortOnValues, Order:=xlDescending, DataOption:=xlSortTextAsNumbers

With .Sort
    .SetRange Range(Selection(1), Selection(Selection.Count))
    .Header = xlGuess
    .MatchCase = False
    .Orientation = xlTopToBottom
    .SortMethod = xlPinYin
    .Apply
End With

.Cells(1, 1).Value = "ソート基準列"
.Cells(1, 3).Value = "(B～C列は計画作成用)"

End With

End Sub

Sub KoteiGP_btn(Optional r As IRibbonControl)


Dim reg As RegExp
Set reg = New RegExp

reg.Pattern = "^(C?-?[0-9A-Z]+?S?)(-[0-9A-Z]+?-?[0-9A-Z]*?)$"


If ActiveSheet.Name <> "job作成" Then
    MsgBox "対応外のシート"
    End
End If


Application.CutCopyMode = False

Dim d As Dictionary
Set d = New Dictionary
Dim D2 As Dictionary
Set D2 = New Dictionary
Dim GP_Sheet As Worksheet
Set GP_Sheet = Sheets("グループ分け")
Dim HinmokuCol As Long
Dim str As String

HinmokuCol = Rows(1).Find("品目", LookIn:=xlValues, LookAt:=xlWhole).Column


Dim i As Long
For i = 2 To GP_Sheet.Cells(GP_Sheet.Rows.Count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    If Not reg.Test(CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 2).Value)) Then
        MsgBox "グループ分けで品目名が非対応の形式が含まれています｡"
        MsgBox CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 2).Value)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    str = reg.Execute(CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 2).Value))(0).SubMatches(0) + reg.Execute(CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 2).Value))(0).SubMatches(1)
    If d.Exists(str) Then
        MsgBox "グループ分けで品目が重複しています｡(｢S-｣有り無しは同一とみなしています)" & vbCrLf & str
        Exit Sub
    Else
        d.add str, CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 1).Value)
    End If
    If Not D2.Exists(CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 1).Value)) Then
        D2.add CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 1).Value), CStr(GP_Sheet.Cells(i, 1).Value)
    End If
Next i


Dim TargetRow As Long
On Error GoTo e
TargetRow = InputBox("挿入行を入力")
If TargetRow < 1 Then Call err.Raise(50000)
On Error GoTo 0

Dim FreeRow As Long
FreeRow = TargetRow

'「作業連絡一覧.xlsx」から転記
'addWorkCom Workbooks("段取り予定.xlsx").Worksheets("job作成"), HinmokuCol, TargetRow

Dim tmp
Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
Dim FirstFlag As Boolean
Dim nameKotei As Range

For Each tmp In D2
    Rows(TargetRow).Insert
    Rows(TargetRow).ClearFormats
    Set nameKotei = Cells(TargetRow, HinmokuCol - 3)
    nameKotei.Value = tmp
    TargetRow = TargetRow + 1
    FirstFlag = True
    
    For i = TargetRow To Cells(Rows.Count, HinmokuCol).End(xlUp).row
        If reg.Test(CStr(Cells(i, HinmokuCol).Value)) Then
            str = reg.Execute(CStr(Cells(i, HinmokuCol).Value))(0).SubMatches(0) + reg.Execute(CStr(Cells(i, HinmokuCol).Value))(0).SubMatches(1)
            If d.Exists(str) Then
                If d(str) = tmp Then
                    If FirstFlag Then
                        With Cells(TargetRow - 1, HinmokuCol - 2)
                            .Value = Format(Cells(i, HinmokuCol + 3).Value, "yymmdd") & "-xxx"
                            .Font.Bold = True
                            .Font.Color = 255
                            .Font.Size = 16
                        End With
                        FirstFlag = False
                    End If
                    Rows(TargetRow).Insert
                    Rows(i + 1).Copy Rows(TargetRow)
                    Rows(i + 1).Delete
                    TargetRow = TargetRow + 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    DoEvents
    Next i
    
    If FirstFlag Then nameKotei.Value = ""

Next tmp

FirstFlag = True
Rows(FreeRow).Insert
Rows(FreeRow).ClearFormats
i = Cells(Rows.Count, HinmokuCol).End(xlUp).row

Do While i > TargetRow
    If reg.Test(CStr(Cells(i, HinmokuCol).Value)) Then
        If FirstFlag Then
            With Cells(FreeRow, HinmokuCol - 2)
                .Value = Format(Cells(i, HinmokuCol + 3).Value, "yymmdd") & "-xxx"
                .Font.Bold = True
                .Font.Color = 255
                .Font.Size = 16
            End With
            FirstFlag = False
        End If
        Rows(FreeRow + 1).Insert
        Rows(i + 1).Copy Rows(FreeRow + 1)
        Rows(i + 1).Delete
    End If
DoEvents
TargetRow = TargetRow + 1

Loop


Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
Application.ScreenUpdating = True



Exit Sub

e:
MsgBox "中断"
Exit Sub

End Sub

VBA MACRO 作業連絡.bas 
in file: C:\Users\ss80751\AppData\Local\Temp\kohden-code\TOOL_vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/作業連絡'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit

' "job作成"シート、品目列の番号、「固定品目移動」の挿入行番号 を渡す
Public Sub addWorkCom(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal HinmokuCol As Long, ByVal target As Long)
    Dim lastRow As Long, i As Long
    Dim workCom As String, contents As String
    Dim wb As Workbook
    
    On Error GoTo Cleanup
    
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wb = Workbooks.Open(Filename:="\\nksv523.ad.nkc.co.jp\第二工場実装課用\1係用\SMTData\作業連絡台帳.xlsx", ReadOnly:=True)
    If wb Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "作業連絡台帳を開けませんでした。"
        GoTo Cleanup
    End If
    On Error GoTo Cleanup
    
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, HinmokuCol).End(xlUp).row
    If target <= lastRow Then
        For i = target To lastRow
            ' target行以降の品目をbuildText関数に渡して"作業連絡【】～"のテキストを作成
            workCom = buildText(wb.Worksheets("作業連絡一覧"), CStr(ws.Cells(i, HinmokuCol).Value & ""), ws.Cells(i, HinmokuCol + 3).Value)
            ' "備考1"欄に何か書いてある場合は改行してから結合する
            If Len(workCom) > 0 Then
                contents = CStr(ws.Cells(i, HinmokuCol - 2).Value & "")
                If Len(Trim$(contents)) > 0 Then contents = contents & vbCrLf
                contents = contents & workCom
                ws.Cells(i, HinmokuCol - 2).Value = contents
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
Cleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not wb Is Nothing Then wb.Close SaveChanges:=False
    Set wb = Nothing
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

' 作業連絡一覧シートからhinmokuに対する作業連絡をすべて検索して"作業連絡【】～"のテキストを作成
Private Function buildText(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal hinmoku As String, ByVal startDate As Variant) As String
    Dim lastRow As Long, r As Long
    Dim b As String, a As String, c As String, d As String
    Dim part As String, res As String
    Dim describe As String
    Dim addMes As Boolean
    Dim forSkip() As Variant
    Dim forRemove() As Variant
    Dim var As Variant
    
    ' Job表記揺れ対策
    forSkip = Array("JOB", "Job", "job", "ジョブ")
    ' 除外文字列(表記揺れ想定)
    forRemove = Array("【MES】", "JOB/", "Job/", "job/", "ジョブ/", "JOB", "Job", "job", "ジョブ")
    
    ' 最終行（B列基準）
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, "B").End(xlUp).row
    If lastRow < 2 Or Len(hinmoku) = 0 Then
        buildText = vbNullString
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' 引数品目名も正規化（S除去）しておく
    Dim keyNorm As String
    keyNorm = RemoveS(Trim$(CStr(hinmoku & "")))
    
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        b = RemoveS(Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, "B").Value & "")))
        If StrComp(b, keyNorm, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            
            c = Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, "C").Value & ""))
            ' C列の値("工程")が "Job" であればスキップ
            For Each var In forSkip
                If StrComp(c, var, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then GoTo NextRow
            Next var
            
            a = Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, "A").Value & ""))
            d = CStr(ws.Cells(r, "D").Value & "")
            
            ' MES 判定
            addMes = (InStr(1, d, "【MES】", vbTextCompare) > 0)
            
            ' D列の値("記載項目") から除外文字列を削除
            For Each var In forRemove
                d = Replace(d, var, "", , , vbTextCompare)
            Next var
            
            ' 2行目の本文 = d とする
            describe = d
            
            ' 1行分を組み立て
            part = "作業連絡【" & a & "】"
            If addMes Then part = part & "【MES】"
            If IsDate(startDate) Then
                If CDate(startDate) > CDate(ws.Cells(r, "F").Value) Then part = part & "※期限"
            End If
            
            ' 2行目（本文）を結合：空でなければ改行して追加
            describe = Trim$(describe)
            If Len(describe) > 0 Then
                part = part & vbCrLf & describe
            End If
            
            ' 連結（複数ヒットを改行で繋ぐ）
            If Len(res) > 0 Then
                res = res & vbCrLf & part
            Else
                res = part
            End If
        End If
NextRow:
    Next r
    
    buildText = res
End Function

' B文字列の「最初のハイフン」の直前が 'S'（大小問わず）なら一文字除去
Private Function RemoveS(ByVal s As String) As String
    Dim pos As Long
    pos = InStr(1, s, "-", vbBinaryCompare)
    If pos > 1 Then
        ' 大小問わず S と見なす
        If UCase$(Mid$(s, pos - 1, 1)) = "S" Then
            RemoveS = Left$(s, pos - 2) & Mid$(s, pos)
            Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    RemoveS = s
End Function
